```
你在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/ 做 B 级系统补齐——**油漆（Paint）系统全量移植**。这是多次登记的"依赖未建系统"遗留（智能光标油漆三策略恒空/Enemy 踩油漆色 lerp 未消费）。用户要求完全忠于原版 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/，全程行号注释，不允许近似。

== 原版调研（先 grep 定位全文再实现）==
1. **数据模型**：Tile.cs 的 paint/-wall 油漆存储语义 + PaintID 全表与 Sets（哪些 tile 不可涂/深层铜银是否覆盖）。
2. **物品族**：油漆刷/滚刷/刮刀 id + 油漆物品 id 清单 + 消耗语义（滚刷一片耗 1?）。
3. **涂刷逻辑**（Player.cs ItemCheck 油漆段）：刷=单格、滚刷=同排连续格、刮刀=清除；tile 与 wall 两通道。
4. **渲染消费**：tile 上色（tileColor 乘色）、Minimap 色换算（ColorOfTile 吃 paint 的行号）、油漆过的墙渲染。
5. **购买**：油漆师商店。

== 我方落点 ==
- TileStore 加 paint/paintWall（Uint8）+ 存档往返（rleTiles 扩展位）。
- 渲染：ChunkCache tile 绘制按 paint 乘色；Minimap LUT 换色（对表 ColorOfTile 行号）。
- 交互：useItem 油漆分支（刷/滚/刮）+滚刷涂排。
- SmartCursor 油漆三策略（Roller/Brush/Scraper）从 no-op 激活（SmartCursor.ts 占位与 :668-807 注释已有）。
- Enemy 踩油漆 lerp（Enemy.ts:936 近似处激活）。
- 油漆师商店接 TownNPC 链。

== 要求 ==
1. 测试 tests/paint.test.ts（≥12 例）。
2. npx tsc 零新增错误 + 新测试全绿 + 全量报告（他人 WIP 失败隔离确认）。
3. 不改无关代码。报告：原版行号+实现清单+等价边界+测试结果。
工作区多代理并发，非你造成的失败隔离确认即可。
```

73	    this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
74	    this.half = new Uint8Array(n);
75	    this.slope = new Uint8Array(n);
76	    this.wire = new Uint8Array(n);
77	  }
78	
79	  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }
80	
81	  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
82	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
83	  }
84	
85	  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }
86	
87	  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
88	    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
89	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
90	      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
91	  }
92	
93	  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
94	  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
95	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
96	  }
97	
98	  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
99	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
100	    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
101	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
102	    return !!d && d.solid;
103	  }
104	
105	  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
106	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
107	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
108	    return !!d && d.platform;
109	  }
110	
111	  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
112	    this.listeners.push(fn);
113	  }
114	
115	  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），
116	   *  只有光照订阅（水的光衰减）——避免水流时每帧冲爆 chunk 重建队列造成卡顿 */
117	  private liquidListeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
118	  onLiquidChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
119	    this.liquidListeners.push(fn);
120	  }
121	
122	  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
123	  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
124	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
125	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
126	    this.type[i] = type;
127	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
128	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
129	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
130	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除清半砖/坡面
131	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetTile, x, y, v: type, fx: frameX, fy: frameY });
132	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
133	  }
134	
135	  /** 运行期改半砖/坡面位（锤子交互用；生成期直接写数组即可） */
136	  setHalfBrick(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
137	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
138	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
139	    this.half[i] = v ? 1 : 0;
140	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
141	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
142	  }
143	
144	  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {
145	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
146	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
147	    this.slope[i] = s;
148	    this.half[i] = 0; // 原版 SlopeTile 无条件 halfBrick(false)（WorldGen.cs:49174），slope=0 复位时同样
149	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetSlope, x, y, v: s, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
150	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
151	  }
152	
153	  setTileSilent(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
154	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
155	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
156	    this.type[i] = type;
157	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
158	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
159	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
160	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除同步清位（防生成期脏残留）
161	  }
162	
163	  setWall(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
164	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
165	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
166	    if (this.wall[i] !== wall) {
167	      this.wall[i] = wall;
168	      if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWall, x, y, v: wall, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
169	      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
170	    }
171	  }
172	
173	  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
174	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
175	    this.wall[this.idx(x, y)] = wall;
176	  }
177	
178	  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
179	   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
180	  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
181	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
182	    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
183	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWire, x, y, v: bits & 63, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
184	  }
185	
186	  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
187	    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
188	  }
189	
190	  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
191	  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
192	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
193	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
194	    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
195	    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
196	    this.wire[i] = b;
197	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
198	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
199	  }
200	
201	  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
202	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
203	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
204	    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
205	    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
206	    this.liquid[i] = a;
207	    this.liquidType[i] = type;
208	    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
209	  }
210	}
211

1	// chunk 静态缓存：每 chunk 16×16 tile 预渲染到离屏 canvas（墙层+tile 层）
2	import { CHUNK, TILE } from '../core/constants';
3	import { T, TILE_DEFS, WALL_DEFS, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../data/tiles';
4	import { hash2 } from '../core/rng';
5	import { drawVanillaCell, drawTreeCell } from './VanillaTiler';
6	import { swayBakeSkip } from './WindSway';

8	import { VanillaWallTiler, wallAnimRate } from './VanillaWallTiler';
9	import { shade } from '../assets/Palette';
10	import type { TileSheetEntry } from '../assets/TileSheetGen';
11	import type { AutoTiler } from './AutoTiler';

13	
14	// 我们的 tile → Maples RuleTile 规则名（有规则的走素材自动贴合，无规则回退程序化）
15	// Grass 走人工标注（'@grass'）：Tiles_2.png 是多 tile 混排图集，自动分类不可靠；
16	// 用 public/annotator.html 标注后写入 sprites/annotations.json。无标注时回退程序化。
17	const TILE_RULES: Record<number, string> = {
18	  // 泥土/石/草走原版 BlendRules；铁矿走原版表——均不再用 Maples 规则
19	  13: '工作台', 14: '熔炉', 15: '铁砧',
20	};
21	
22	export interface ChunkPair {
23	  wall: HTMLCanvasElement;   // 背景墙层（水画在它之上）
24	  tile: HTMLCanvasElement;   // 前景 tile/物体层（画在水之上）
25	}
26	
27	export class ChunkCache {
28	  chunks = new Map<number, ChunkPair>();
29	  dirtyQueue: number[] = [];
30	  sheets: Map<number, TileSheetEntry>;
31	  world: World;
32	  autotiler: AutoTiler | null;
33	  wallTiler: VanillaWallTiler | null;
34	  truncatesWalls: number[] = [];
35	  /** 含动画 tile 的 chunk，按 sheet 分组（换帧时只重建对应 chunk，避免全量重烘焙） */
36	  private animChunksBySheet = new Map<number, Set<number>>();
37	  /** 含动画墙的 chunk，按 wallId 分组（墙无 sheet 概念；DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 的
38	   *  11 类换带墙 + 242/243 星彩玻璃逐格错相——换带时只重建对应 chunk） */
39	  private animChunksByWall = new Map<number, Set<number>>();
40	  /** LRU 上限:每 chunk 2×256² canvas = 512KB;384 chunk ≈ 196MB(缩放 0.5 时
41	   *  可视 ~100 chunk 仍绰绰有余)。此前 Map 只增不减——跑图积累无界(内存泄漏 #1) */
42	  static readonly MAX_CHUNKS = 384;
43	  /** 最近一次 flushDirty 实测耗时 ms（F5 调试报告：烘焙尖峰证据面） */
44	  lastFlushMs = 0;
45	  lastFlushCount = 0;
46	
47	  /** 释放全部 chunk 画布 GPU 背板并清表(退出世界必须调用)。
48	   *  detached canvas 的回收依赖 GC 且明显滞后——连续多次读档累积数百 MB
49	   *  显存,最终 contextlost/contextrestored 风暴卡死(2026-08-10 trace 实证) */
50	  dispose(): void {
51	    for (const pair of this.chunks.values()) {
52	      pair.wall.width = 0; pair.wall.height = 0;
53	      pair.tile.width = 0; pair.tile.height = 0;
54	    }
55	    this.chunks.clear();
56	    this.dirtyQueue.length = 0;
57	    this.animChunksBySheet.clear();
58	    this.animChunksByWall.clear();
59	  }
60	
61	  constructor(world: World, sheets: Map<number, TileSheetEntry>, autotiler: AutoTiler | null = null, wallTiler: VanillaWallTiler | null = null) {
62	    this.world = world;
63	    this.sheets = sheets;
64	    this.autotiler = autotiler;
65	    this.wallTiler = wallTiler;
66	    // 原版 TileID.Sets.TruncatesWalls（54 玻璃 / 328 暗影宝箱 / 459 / 748）→ 内部 tile id
67	    this.truncatesWalls = ['glass', 'v_328_shadow_chest', 'v_459_', 'v_748_']
68	      .map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k] ?? -1)
69	      .filter((id) => id >= 0);
70	    world.store.onTileChanged((x, y) => this.markDirtyAround(x, y));
71	  }
72	
73	  static key(cx: number, cy: number): number {
74	    return (cx & 0xffff) | ((cy & 0xffff) << 16);
75	  }
76	
77	  markDirty(cx: number, cy: number) {
78	    const k = ChunkCache.key(cx, cy);
79	    if (!this.chunks.has(k)) return; // 未生成的 chunk 惰性创建
80	    this.chunks.set(k, undefined as unknown as ChunkPair); // 标记删除，重绘时重建
81	    if (!this.dirtyQueue.includes(k)) this.dirtyQueue.push(k);
82	  }
83	
84	  /** 区域标脏（tile 范围）：供树冠等大范围精灵清理使用 */
85	  markDirtyArea(x0: number, y0: number, x1: number, y1: number) {
86	    for (let cy = Math.floor(y0 / CHUNK); cy <= Math.floor(y1 / CHUNK); cy++) {
87	      for (let cx = Math.floor(x0 / CHUNK); cx <= Math.floor(x1 / CHUNK); cx++) {
88	        if (cx < 0 || cy < 0) continue;
89	        this.markDirty(cx, cy);
90	      }
91	    }
92	  }
93	
94	  markDirtyAround(x: number, y: number) {
95	    const cx = Math.floor(x / CHUNK), cy = Math.floor(y / CHUNK);
96	    this.markDirty(cx, cy);
97	    // 边缘融合：邻接 chunk 也要标脏
98	    if (x % CHUNK === 0) this.markDirty(cx - 1, cy);
99	    if (x % CHUNK === CHUNK - 1) this.markDirty(cx + 1, cy);
100	    if (y % CHUNK === 0) this.markDirty(cx, cy - 1);
101	    if (y % CHUNK === CHUNK - 1) this.markDirty(cx, cy + 1);
102	  }
103	
104	  /** 全量标脏(atlas 懒加载晚到的新表 → 已烘焙的 chunk 里可能烤了 fallback)。
105	   *  4/帧 的 flushDirty 会逐步重烘焙,dirtyQueue.includes 去重防重复入队 */
106	  invalidateAll(): void {
107	    for (const k of this.chunks.keys()) {
108	      if (this.chunks.get(k) === undefined) continue; // 已是待重建哨兵
109	      this.chunks.set(k, undefined as unknown as ChunkPair);
110	      if (!this.dirtyQueue.includes(k)) this.dirtyQueue.push(k);
111	    }
112	  }
113	
114	  /** 每帧重绘脏 chunk:数量上限 maxN 之外再加时间预算 budgetMs——
115	   *  跑图/全量标脏时烘焙突发不再挤占帧预算(实测 87ms 尖峰来源) */
116	  flushDirty(maxN = 4, budgetMs = 6) {
117	    let n = 0;
118	    const t0 = performance.now();
119	    while (this.dirtyQueue.length && n < maxN) {
120	      const k = this.dirtyQueue.shift()!;
121	      const cx = k & 0xffff, cy = (k >> 16) & 0xffff;
122	      if (this.chunks.get(k) !== undefined) continue; // 已重建
123	      this.get(cx, cy);
124	      n++;
125	      if (performance.now() - t0 > budgetMs) break; // 单 chunk 烘焙超预算也至少完成 1 个
126	    }
127	    this.lastFlushMs = performance.now() - t0;
128	    this.lastFlushCount = n;
129	  }
130	
131	  /** 动画时钟推进（Game 每帧调用）：sheet/wallId 到达换帧行 tick → 只重建对应 chunk。
132	   *  原版语义 = AnimateTiles / DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 每 rate tick 推进一帧；
133	   *  帧内 chunk 复用零开销 */
134	  advanceAnim(): void {
135	    tileAnim.tick++;
136	    if (this.animChunksBySheet.size) {
137	      for (const [sheet, set] of this.animChunksBySheet) {
138	        const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
139	        if (!rate || tileAnim.tick % rate !== 0) continue;
140	        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);
141	      }
142	    }
143	    if (this.animChunksByWall.size) {
144	      for (const [wallId, set] of this.animChunksByWall) {
145	        const rate = wallAnimRate(wallId);
146	        if (!rate || tileAnim.tick % rate !== 0) continue;
147	        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);
148	      }
149	    }
150	  }
151	
152	  /** 取 chunk 双层画布（惰性生成，LRU 淘汰最久未用） */
153	  get(cx: number, cy: number): ChunkPair {
154	    const k = ChunkCache.key(cx, cy);
155	    let c = this.chunks.get(k);
156	    if (c) {
157	      // LRU:命中即刷新 recency(delete+set 移到 Map 尾部)。渲染循环每帧都 get
158	      // 可视 chunk,故屏上 chunk 永远最新、不会被误淘汰
159	      this.chunks.delete(k);
160	      this.chunks.set(k, c);
161	      return c;
162	    }
163	    c = this.renderChunk(cx, cy);
164	    this.chunks.set(k, c);
165	    while (this.chunks.size > ChunkCache.MAX_CHUNKS) {
166	      const oldest = this.chunks.keys().next().value as number | undefined;
167	      if (oldest === undefined) break;
168	      this.chunks.delete(oldest);
169	    }
170	    return c;
171	  }
172	
173	  /** 树枝判定：TREE 且上下皆非 TREE、恰好一侧为 TREE（横向独连树干）。
174	   *  下方是实心地面的属于树根底座 —— 走规则表渲染底座贴图，不算枝干 */
175	
176	  private neighborMask(x: number, y: number, type: number): number {
177	    const st = this.world.store;
178	    let mask = 0;
179	    const same = (nx: number, ny: number) => st.inBounds(nx, ny) && st.flags[st.idx(nx, ny)] && st.type[st.idx(nx, ny)] === type ? 1 : 0;
180	    mask |= same(x, y - 1);        // N
181	    mask |= same(x + 1, y) << 1;   // E
182	    mask |= same(x, y + 1) << 2;   // S
183	    mask |= same(x - 1, y) << 3;   // W
184	    mask |= same(x + 1, y - 1) << 4; // NE
185	    mask |= same(x + 1, y + 1) << 5; // SE
186	    mask |= same(x - 1, y + 1) << 6; // SW
187	    mask |= same(x - 1, y - 1) << 7; // NW
188	    return mask;
189	  }
190	
191	  private renderChunk(cx: number, cy: number): ChunkPair {
192	    // 双层画布：墙层 / tile 层分离 —— 水渲染在两层之间（墙之上、图块之下）
193	    const wall = document.createElement('canvas');
194	    wall.width = CHUNK * TILE; wall.height = CHUNK * TILE;
195	    const tile = document.createElement('canvas');
196	    tile.width = CHUNK * TILE; tile.height = CHUNK * TILE;
197	    let ctx = wall.getContext('2d')!;
198	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
199	    const st = this.world.store;
200	    const x0 = cx * CHUNK, y0 = cy * CHUNK;
201	
202	    // ---- 第一遍：背景墙全部先画（避免后格的墙盖住跨格物体如宝箱/树冠）----
203	    // 原版墙 framing（VanillaWallTiler）：32×32 帧以格为中心外溢 8px →
204	    // 扫描范围外扩 1 格，跨 chunk 边界的帧由相邻 chunk 补齐（像素一致无副作用）
205	    if (this.wallTiler) {
206	      const EXT = 1;
207	      for (let ly = -EXT; ly < CHUNK + EXT; ly++) {
208	        for (let lx = -EXT; lx < CHUNK + EXT; lx++) {
209	          const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;
210	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
211	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
212	          const wallId = st.wall[i];
213	          if (wallId === 0) continue;
214	          const px = lx * TILE, py = ly * TILE;
215	          if (this.wallTiler.hasTexture(wallId)) {
216	            this.wallTiler.draw(ctx, st, x, y, wallId, this.truncatesWalls, px, py);
217	            // 动画墙（DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 换带 + 星彩玻璃逐格错相）：登记进换带
218	            // 重烘焙行列——墙无 sheet 概念，按 wallId 另建 map（tiles 侧同款机制）
219	            if (wallAnimRate(wallId) !== 0) {
220	              let wset = this.animChunksByWall.get(wallId);
221	              if (!wset) { wset = new Set(); this.animChunksByWall.set(wallId, wset); }
222	              wset.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
223	            }
224	          } else {
225	            const wd = WALL_DEFS[wallId];
226	            if (wd) {
227	              ctx.fillStyle = wd.mapColor;
228	              ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE);
229	              ctx.fillStyle = shade(wd.mapColor, 0.8);
230	              ctx.fillRect(px, py + TILE - 1, TILE, 1);
231	              ctx.fillRect(px + TILE - 1, py, 1, TILE);
232	            }
233	          }
234	        }
235	      }
236	    }
237	
238	    // ---- 第二遍：前景 tile / 物体（绘制到 tile 层画布；水渲染在墙层与 tile 层之间）----
239	    ctx = tile.getContext('2d')!;
240	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
241	    for (let ly = 0; ly < CHUNK; ly++) {
242	      for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {
243	        const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;
244	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
245	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
246	        const px = lx * TILE, py = ly * TILE;
247	        const type = st.type[i];
248	        // 原版语义:非活性格不渲染(TileRunner 会给空气格写幽灵 type)
249	        if (type === 0 || !st.flags[i]) continue;
250	        // 已致动(inActive):幽灵态淡显(原版 DrawInactiveSorter,Main.cs:2828 附近)
251	        // 每格开头统一设定 alpha(各 continue 路径无需逐个恢复,下一格自愈)
252	        const actuated = (st.wire[i] & 32) !== 0;
253	        ctx.globalAlpha = actuated ? 0.3 : 1;
254	        const def = TILE_DEFS[type];
255	        if (!def) { ctx.fillStyle = '#808080'; ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE); continue; }
256	        // 风摆动图块（草/藤/吊挂植物/树冠标记帧）：摘出静态烘焙，
257	        // 由 Renderer 的 WindSway overlay 逐帧动态绘制（原版 AddSpecialPoint 特殊路径）
258	        if (def.vanilla && swayBakeSkip(type, st.frameX[i])) continue;
259	        // 原版素材图块（TileDef.vanilla）：TEdit framing 查找表（auto）或显式 18px 帧（style）
260	        if (def.vanilla && this.autotiler) {
261	          // 动画陈设（原版 AnimateTiles/GetTileDrawData addFrY）：frameY += 帧索引*pitch
262	          // （pitch 默认 38，3 格高特例组 54，篝火族特例 36+熄灭行静止——见 TileAnim）
263	          let fy = st.frameY[i];
264	          if (def.vanilla.sheet === 215) {
265	            fy += campfireYOffset(st.frameY[i]);
266	            let set215 = this.animChunksBySheet.get(215);
267	            if (!set215) { set215 = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(215, set215); }
268	            set215.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
269	          } else if (def.vanilla.sheet === 314) {
270	            // 矿车轨道加速带动画（Main.cs:18734-18741 每 10 tick 推进、5 帧回卷）：
271	            // 只注册重烘焙——frameY 是后轨连接 ID，勿走 addFrY 帧偏移；
272	            // 帧行偏移由 drawMinecartTrackCell → sourceRectOf(frameID, anim) 处理
273	            let set314 = this.animChunksBySheet.get(314);
274	            if (!set314) { set314 = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(314, set314); }
275	            set314.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
276	          } else if (TILE_ANIM_RATE[def.vanilla.sheet]) {
277	            const rows = this.autotiler.atlas.vmeta(def.vanilla.sheet)?.rows ?? 0;
278	            fy += animYOffset(def.vanilla.sheet, rows * 18);
279	            let set = this.animChunksBySheet.get(def.vanilla.sheet);
280	            if (!set) { set = new Set(); this.animChunksBySheet.set(def.vanilla.sheet, set); }
281	            set.add(ChunkCache.key(cx, cy));
282	          }
283	          drawVanillaCell(
284	            ctx, this.autotiler.atlas, def.vanilla.sheet, def.vanilla.frame,
285	            def.vanilla.fw ?? 1, def.vanilla.fh ?? 1,
286	            st, x, y, type,
287	            (t) => t === type, // 同 id 融合判定（后续可扩 mergeWith）
288	            px, py, st.frameX[i], fy,
289	            { treeX: this.world.treeX, treeStyle: this.world.treeStyle, treeTops: this.world.treeTops,
290	              worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, worldW: this.world.w },
291	          );
292	          continue;
293	        }
294	        // 树苗：Tree_Bodys 树干段作小苗（底部对齐）
295	        if (type === T.SAPLING && this.autotiler) {
296	          const r = this.autotiler.saplingSprite(x, y);
297	          if (r) {
298	            ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, px + (TILE - r.sw) / 2, py + TILE - r.sh, r.sw, r.sh);
299	            continue;
300	          }
301	        }
302	        // 杂草：Maples Tiles_3 杂草贴图（16×20，底部对齐，hash 选变体）
303	        if (type === T.TALLGRASS && this.autotiler) {
304	          const r = this.autotiler.weedSprite(x, y);
305	          if (r) {
306	            ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, px - (r.sw - TILE) / 2, py + TILE - r.sh, r.sw, r.sh);
307	            continue;
308	          }
309	        }
310	        // 有 RuleTile 规则的 tile 用 Maples 素材自动贴合
311	        const ruleName = this.autotiler ? TILE_RULES[type] : undefined;
312	        if (ruleName && this.autotiler) {
313	          // 草皮覆盖件：保持原生透明（缺口露出背后的墙/天空），不做任何垫底/填充
314	          if (ruleName === '@grass') {
315	            const r = this.autotiler.tile(ruleName, st, x, y, type);
316	            if (r) ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, px, py, TILE, TILE);
317	            continue;
318	          }
319	          const r = this.autotiler.tile(ruleName, st, x, y, type);
320	          if (r) {
321	            // 大图（树冠 80×80，宽>2格）跳过 —— 第三遍统一绘制（跨 chunk 补全 + 树叶盖树干）
322	            if (r.sw > TILE * 2) continue;
323	            // 按精灵原始尺寸绘制。树干等"宽≤2格、高>1格"的竖向件顶部对齐：
324	            // 溢出向下伸，由更下方的格（后画）覆盖 —— 下层不压上层。
325	            let dy = py + (TILE - r.sh) / 2;
326	            if (r.sh > TILE && r.sw <= TILE * 2) dy = py;
327	            const dx = px + (TILE - r.sw) / 2;
328	            ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, dx, dy, r.sw, r.sh);
329	            continue;
330	          }
331	        }
332	        const entry = this.sheets.get(type);
333	        if (!entry) {
334	          ctx.fillStyle = '#FF00FF';
335	          ctx.fillRect(px, py, TILE, TILE);
336	          continue;
337	        }
338	        if (entry.kind === 'blend') {
339	          const mask = this.neighborMask(x, y, type);
340	          const col = mask & 15, row = mask >> 4;
341	          ctx.drawImage(entry.canvas, col * TILE, row * TILE, TILE, TILE, px, py, TILE, TILE);
342	        } else if (entry.kind === 'object') {
343	          // 仅锚点绘制整体
344	          if (st.frameX[i] === 0 && st.frameY[i] === 0) {
345	            const w = (entry.w ?? 1) * TILE, h = (entry.h ?? 1) * TILE;
346	            ctx.drawImage(entry.canvas, px, py, w, h);
347	          }
348	        } else {
349	          // single：帧偏移直接取
350	          ctx.drawImage(entry.canvas, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i], TILE, TILE, px, py, TILE, TILE);
351	        }
352	      }
353	    }
354	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; // 致动幽灵 alpha 复位(防影响后续 pass)
355	
356	    // ---- 第三遍：半砖（halfBrick）——主绘制后清掉上半 8px ----
357	    // VanillaTiler blend/auto/style 三路径已按原版源矩形裁剪（源 y+8 高-8）；
358	    // 此处 clearRect 仅作兜底（uv 查找失败走 vframe(1,1) 全帧回退等路径仍画满 16×16）
359	    for (let ly = 0; ly < CHUNK; ly++) {
360	      for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {
361	        const i = st.idx(x0 + lx, y0 + ly);
362	        if (st.half[i]) ctx.clearRect(lx * TILE, ly * TILE, TILE, 8);
363	      }
364	    }
365	
366	    // ---- 第四遍：树静态部分（跨 chunk 外扩绘制） ----
367	    // 风摆动系统接管后：树冠/树枝标记帧不再烘焙（Renderer WindSway overlay 逐帧摆动），
368	    // 本遍只保留棕榈干身（倾斜跨列必须外扩遍）与蘑菇树顶（72 原版不摆动）。
369	    if (this.autotiler) {
370	      const treeIds = ['v_72_mushroom_tree', 'v_323_palm_trees']
371	        .map((k) => TILE_BY_KEY[k]).filter((id) => id !== undefined);
372	      for (const v5 of treeIds) {
373	        const EXT = 6;
374	        for (let ty = y0 - EXT; ty < y0 + CHUNK + EXT; ty++) {
375	          for (let tx = x0 - EXT; tx < x0 + CHUNK + EXT; tx++) {
376	            if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
377	            const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
378	            if (st.type[i] !== v5) continue;
379	            const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
380	            const def = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
381	            const sheet = def?.vanilla?.sheet ?? 5;
382	            const trees = { treeX: this.world.treeX, treeStyle: this.world.treeStyle, treeTops: this.world.treeTops,
383	              worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, worldW: this.world.w };
384	            if (sheet === 323) {
385	              const isCrown = fx >= 88 && fx <= 132;
386	              if (isCrown) continue; // 棕榈冠 → WindSway overlay
387	              // 棕榈干身（倾斜跨列，必须外扩遍）
388	              drawTreeCell(ctx, this.autotiler.atlas, st, tx, ty, fx, fy,
389	                tx * TILE - x0 * TILE, ty * TILE - y0 * TILE, sheet, false, trees);
390	            } else {
391	              // 蘑菇树：顶格标记帧原版不摆动，保留烘焙（DrawTrees switch 无 case 72）
392	              drawTreeCell(ctx, this.autotiler.atlas, st, tx, ty, fx, fy,
393	                tx * TILE - x0 * TILE, ty * TILE - y0 * TILE, sheet, true, trees);
394	            }
395	          }
396	        }
397	      }
398	    }
399	
400	    return { wall, tile };
401	  }
402	}
403

3	// 性能(2026-08 审计 #2):RLE 输出为密度增长 Uint8Array(varint 字节流)。
4	// 【v3 稳定 id 方案】磁盘 tile/item id 不再存数组下标:TILE_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL /
5	// ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL 内联映射(data/tiles.ts + data/items.ts 末段,根治顺序漂移)。
6	
7	import { TILE_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL } from '../data/tiles';
8	import { ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL } from '../data/items';
9	import type { WeatherSave } from '../world/weather/Weather';
10	import type { FurnitureSaveEntry } from '../world/FurnitureItems';
11	
12	class ByteWriter {
13	  buf = new Uint8Array(1 << 16);
14	  len = 0;
15	  private grow(need: number) {
16	    if (this.len + need <= this.buf.length) return;
17	    let cap = this.buf.length;
18	    while (cap < this.len + need) cap *= 2;
19	    const nb = new Uint8Array(cap);
20	    nb.set(this.buf.subarray(0, this.len));
21	    this.buf = nb;
22	  }
23	  varint(v: number) {
24	    v = v < 0 ? 0 : v;
25	    this.grow(5);  // 32 位 varint 至多 5 字节
26	    while (v >= 0x80) {
27	      this.buf[this.len++] = (v & 0x7f) | 0x80;
28	      v >>>= 7;
29	    }
30	    this.buf[this.len++] = v;
31	  }
32	  bytes(): Uint8Array {
33	    return this.buf.subarray(0, this.len);
34	  }
35	}
36	
37	export function bytesToB64(u8: Uint8Array): string {
38	  // 分块拼接(8192)避免逐字符 += 的 ConsString 链;输出与逐字符版一致
39	  let s = '';
40	  const CH = 8192;
41	  for (let i = 0; i < u8.length; i += CH) {
42	    s += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, u8.subarray(i, Math.min(i + CH, u8.length)) as unknown as number[]);
43	  }
44	  return btoa(s);
45	}
46	
47	// ---- 正式 RLE 格式 ----
48	// tiles 每条目: varint(run-1) varint(type) [varint(frameX) varint(frameY) 仅当 type!=0]
49	// walls/liquid: varint(run-1) varint(value)
50	
51	export interface SaveData {
52	  format: 'sandboxworld.save';
53	  version: number;
54	  /** v3: tile/item 磁盘 id = 原版 sheet/item id + 私有段(见 data/tiles.ts 稳定表);v2=旧内部下标 */
55	  idScheme?: 'sheet';
56	  header: {
57	    name: string; seed: number; width: number; height: number;
58	    spawn: [number, number]; groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number; lavaLine?: number;
59	    crimson?: boolean; dungeonX?: number; dungeonY?: number; jungleX?: number;
60	    treeX?: number[]; treeStyle?: number[]; treeTops?: number[];
61	    created: number; playTimeMs: number;

64	    seedFlags?: Record<string, boolean>; // 彩蛋种子旗标（旧档缺省={}）
65	    /** 世界难度 = Main.GameMode（Main.cs:2677：0 经典 1 专家 2 大师 3 旅程；
66	     *  原版随 WorldFileData 持久化；旧档缺省 0=经典） */
67	    difficulty?: number;
68	    /** 旅程时间倍率（CreativePowers.ModifyTimeRate.TargetTimeRate，CreativePowers.cs:866-884；
69	     *  IPersistentPerWorldContent 随世界存档；旧档缺省 1） */
70	    journeyTimeRate?: number;
71	    /** 旅程时间冻结（CreativePowers.FreezeTime，CreativePowers.cs:1448；旧档缺省 false） */
72	    journeyFreezeTime?: boolean;
73	    /** 日晷冷却/快进到黎明（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342；旧档缺省 0/false） */
74	    sundialCooldown?: number; fastForwardTimeToDawn?: boolean;
75	    /** 月晷冷却/快进到黄昏（WorldFile.cs:1416-1417；旧档缺省 0/false） */
76	    moondialCooldown?: number; fastForwardTimeToDusk?: boolean;
77	  };
78	  clock: {
79	    timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number;
80	    /** 原版 header _tempBloodMoon/_tempEclipse/_tempMoonPhase（WF:1276-1278,读写血月/日食/月相;
81	     *  旧档缺省=false/0/0——血月按夜重掷、月相回满月） */
82	    bloodMoon?: boolean; eclipse?: boolean; moonPhase?: number;
83	  };
84	  /** 入侵状态（原版 WorldFile.cs:1306-1339 五元组；旧档缺省=无入侵） */
85	  invasion?: { type: number; size: number; sizeStart: number; x: number; delay: number };
86	  /** 已砸祭坛数（WorldGen.altarCount，WorldFile.cs:1303/2100；旧档缺省 0） */
87	  altarCount?: number;
88	  /** 流星雨剩余计数（WorldGen.meteorShowerCount，WorldFile.cs:1069/:177；旧档缺省 0） */
89	  meteorShowerCount?: number;
90	  /** 夜间小动物概率五件套（NPC.setFireFlyChance，NPC.cs:94557-94610；每夜重掷，
91	   *  原版为 NPC 静态位不落盘——本仓按运行时审计台账要求随存档；旧档缺省=代表值） */
92	  critterChances?: {
93	    fireFly: number; fireFlyFriendly: number; fireFlyMultiple: number;
94	    butterfly: number; stinkBug: number;
95	  };
96	  /** CultistRitual.delay（CultistRitual.cs:32，WorldFile.cs:1340 写/:2192 读；
97	   *  旧档缺省 86400 = delayStart，v108 前原版旧档同值 WF:2188） */

100	   *  2147-2161；anglerQuestFinished 运行时态不存——读档 loadTempInfo 重置 WF:628-629） */
101	  angler?: { quest: number; whoFinishedToday: string[] };
102	  /** 困难矿世界锁定选种（WorldGen.SavedOreTiers，WF:1305-1307；旧档缺省 -1 = 未砸过） */

107	  walls: string;
108	  liquid: string;
109	  liquidType: string;  // base64 RLE（0无 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜 4微光）
110	  wire?: string;       // base64 RLE 电路位（bit0-3 四色线 bit4 致动器 bit5 已致动；旧档缺此字段=全 0）
111	  blocks?: string;     // base64 RLE 半砖/坡面（0=整砖 1=半砖 2-5=slope1-4;旧档缺=全整砖）
112	  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>; locked?: boolean }>;

114	    hp: number; x: number; y: number;
115	    inventory: Array<SavedItem | null>;
116	    selected: number;
117	    baseMaxHp?: number;   // 生命上限（水晶之心/生命果扩容；旧档缺省 100）
118	    baseMaxMana?: number; // 魔力上限（魔力水晶扩容；旧档缺省 20）
119	    mana?: number;        // 当前魔力
120	    banks?: Array<Array<SavedItem | null>>; // 玩家储物×4（29/97/463/491；旧档缺省全空）
121	    /** armor[20]（0-2 盔甲/3-9 配饰/10-12 时装盔甲/13-19 时装配饰）——旧档缺省经 migrateLegacy 从旧 54 槽布局迁出 */
122	    armor?: Array<SavedItem | null>;

130	    currentLoadout?: number;
131	    /** Buff 槽（原版 buffType+buffTime 44 槽,PL:53913-53925;[vanillaBuffId, 剩余秒],
132	     *  Main.buffNoSave 已过滤——旧档缺省=无 buff） */
133	    buffs?: Array<[number, number]>;
134	    /** 税务员累积税款（Player.taxMoney 铜币,PL:53839;旧档缺省 0） */
135	    taxMoney?: number;
136	    /** 高尔夫累计分（Player.golferScoreAccumulated :1792;旧档缺省 0） */
137	    golferScoreAccumulated?: number;
138	    /** 渔夫任务完成数（Player.anglerQuestsFinished :1790;旧档缺省 0——奖励阶梯据此） */
139	    anglerQuestsFinished?: number;
140	    /** PVE 死亡计数（Player.numberOfDeathsPVE,PL:53840;旧档缺省 0） */
141	    deathsPve?: number;
142	    /** 永久升级族(Player.cs:44575-44802;旧档缺省全 false) */
143	    permanentUpgrades?: {
144	      extraAccessory?: boolean;
145	      usedAegisCrystal?: boolean;
146	      usedAegisFruit?: boolean;
147	      usedArcaneCrystal?: boolean;
148	      usedGummyWorm?: boolean;
149	      usedAmbrosia?: boolean;
150	    };
151	  };
152	  npcs: Array<{ key: string; x: number; y: number; home: [number, number] | null; bound?: boolean; givenName?: string }>;
153	  signs?: Array<{ x: number; y: number; text: string }>; // 墓碑碑文（旧档缺省无）
154	  /** 世界事件态（原版 header 内 party/灯笼夜段,WF:1361-1368/1381-1384;旧档缺省=无派对无预约） */
155	  events?: {
156	    partyManual: boolean;            // BirthdayParty.ManualParty
157	    partyCooldown?: number;         // BirthdayParty.PartyDaysOnCooldown（旧档缺省 0）
158	    lanternManual: boolean;          // 服务端手动灯笼（本仓无入口,位保留）
159	    lanternGenuine: boolean;         // 真灯笼夜兑现中
160	    lanternNext: boolean;            // 次夜灯笼夜预约
161	    lanternCooldown: number;         // 冷却夜数
162	  };
163	  /** 挂物族存放物（物品框 395/武器架 471/模特 470/帽架 475/食物盘 520/展示罐 698；
164	   *  原版 TileEntity 段的最小等价，旧档缺省无） */
165	  furniture?: FurnitureSaveEntry[];
166	  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
167	  weather?: WeatherSave; // 天气快照（雨/风/云/沙尘暴；旧档缺省=晴）
168	  /** 图鉴统计（BestiaryUnlocksTracker——原版随世界文件持久化 WorldFile.cs:3399/3405；
169	   *  旧档缺省=空三桶） */
170	  bestiary?: import('../data/Bestiary').BestiarySave;
171	}

180	/** 序列化所需的 tilestore 视图(worker 路径=副本包装;主线程=活存储) */
181	export interface SaveStoreView {
182	  type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array;
183	  frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array;
184	  wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array; liquidType: Uint8Array; wire: Uint8Array;
185	  half: Uint8Array; slope: Uint8Array;

187	/** 序列化所需的世界元数据(全部可结构化克隆) */
188	export interface SaveMeta {
189	  name: string; seed: number; w: number; h: number;
190	  spawnX: number; spawnY: number; groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number; lavaLine?: number;
191	  treeX?: number[]; treeStyle?: number[]; treeTops?: number[];
192	  crimson?: boolean; dungeonX?: number; dungeonY?: number; jungleX?: number;
193	  timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number;
194	  bloodMoon?: boolean; eclipse?: boolean; moonPhase?: number; moonType?: number;
195	  seedFlags?: Record<string, boolean>;
196	  /** 世界难度 = Main.GameMode（Main.cs:2677；旧档缺省 0=经典） */
197	  difficulty?: number;
198	  /** 旅程时间倍率/冻结（CreativePowers.cs:866/:1448；旧档缺省 1/false） */
199	  journeyTimeRate?: number;
200	  journeyFreezeTime?: boolean;
201	  /** 日晷/月晷快进四件套（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342/1416-1417；旧档缺省 0/0/false/false） */
202	  sundialCooldown?: number; moondialCooldown?: number;
203	  fastForwardTimeToDawn?: boolean; fastForwardTimeToDusk?: boolean;
204	  chests: SaveData['chests']; signs?: SaveData['signs']; furniture?: FurnitureSaveEntry[]; flags: Record<string, boolean>;
205	  weather?: WeatherSave;
206	  invasion?: { type: number; size: number; sizeStart: number; x: number; delay: number };
207	  /** 已砸祭坛数（WorldGen.altarCount，WorldFile.cs:1303/2100；旧档缺省 0） */
208	  altarCount?: number;
209	  /** 流星雨剩余计数（WorldGen.meteorShowerCount，WorldFile.cs:1069/:177；旧档缺省 0） */
210	  meteorShowerCount?: number;
211	  /** 夜间小动物概率五件套（setFireFlyChance；SaveData.critterChances 同形） */
212	  critterChances?: {
213	    fireFly: number; fireFlyFriendly: number; fireFlyMultiple: number;
214	    butterfly: number; stinkBug: number;
215	  };
216	  /** CultistRitual.delay（WF:1340/:2192；缺省 86400 = delayStart） */
217	  cultistDelay?: number;
218	  /** 渔夫任务链世界段（SaveData.angler 同形；旧档缺省=索引 0/空名单） */
219	  angler?: { quest: number; whoFinishedToday: string[] };
220	  /** 困难矿世界锁定选种（WorldGen.SavedOreTiers，WF:1305-1307；旧档缺省 -1 = 未砸过） */
221	  savedOreTiers?: { cobalt: number; mythril: number; adamantite: number };
222	  /** 已砸暗影珠/猩红之心计数（WorldGen.shadowOrbCount，WF:1302/2099；旧档缺省 0） */
223	  shadowOrbCount?: number;
224	  /** 图鉴三桶（杀数/遇数/聊天）——data/Bestiary.ts BestiarySave */
225	  bestiary?: import('../data/Bestiary').BestiarySave;
226	}
227	
228	export function rlePairs(values: ArrayLike<number>): Uint8Array {
229	  const out = new ByteWriter();
230	  const n = values.length;
231	  let i = 0;
232	  while (i < n) {
233	    const v = values[i];
234	    let run = 1;
235	    while (i + run < n && values[i + run] === v && run < 0x7fffffff) run++;
236	    out.varint(run - 1);
237	    out.varint(v);
238	    i += run;
239	  }
240	  return out.bytes();
241	}
242	
243	/** RLE tiles。stableMap 非空时逐元素映射为稳定 id(v3 存档路径) */
244	export function rleTiles(st: { type: Uint16Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array }, stableMap?: Uint16Array): Uint8Array {
245	  const out = new ByteWriter();
246	  const n = st.type.length;
247	  let i = 0;
248	  while (i < n) {
249	    const raw = st.type[i];
250	    const t = stableMap ? (raw === 0 ? 0 : stableMap[raw]) : raw;
251	    const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
252	    let run = 1;
253	    // 游程比较必须 raw 对 raw（t 是映射后的稳定 id，与原始 id 比较恒不等 →
254	    // 自定义 tile（stable≠internal）游程全断，4200x1200 规则地形 17B 膨胀到 21MB）
255	    while (i + run < n && st.type[i + run] === raw && st.frameX[i + run] === fx && st.frameY[i + run] === fy) run++;
256	    out.varint(run - 1);
257	    out.varint(t);
258	    if (t !== 0) {
259	      out.varint(fx);
260	      out.varint(fy);
261	    }
262	    i += run;
263	  }
264	  return out.bytes();
265	}
266	
267	/** 存档序列化主体。幽灵净化在视图上就地执行(主线程=活存储原语义;
268	 * worker=副本,净化不影响游戏内状态,存档字节一致) */
269	export function serializeSave(
270	  st: SaveStoreView, meta: SaveMeta,
271	  player: SaveData['player'], playTimeMs: number,
272	  townNpcs?: SaveData['npcs'],
273	  events?: SaveData['events'],
274	): string {
275	  // 幽灵净化:存档格式不携带 active 位(加载时 type≠0 即激活),
276	  // 非活性格上的残留 type 必须归零,否则重读后全部变成浮空实体块
277	  for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i++) {
278	    if (!st.flags[i] && st.type[i] !== 0) st.type[i] = 0;
279	  }
280	  // 物品 id → 稳定 id(全槽位统一;量小直接遍历)。保留 favorited/prefix。
281	  // v4 起 armor/dye/trash/miscEquips/miscDyes/loadouts 也走稳定 id(v3 曾裸透传内部
282	  // 下标——注册表一变即错位;本地物品退役时顺手根治)
283	  const mapItem = (it: { id: number; stack: number; favorited?: boolean; prefix?: number } | null) =>
284	    it
285	      ? {
286	        id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[it.id] ?? 0,
287	        stack: it.stack,
288	        favorited: it.favorited || undefined,
289	        prefix: it.prefix || undefined,
290	      }
291	      : null;
292	  const data: SaveData = {
293	    format: 'sandboxworld.save',
294	    version: 4,
295	    header: {
296	      name: meta.name, seed: meta.seed, width: meta.w, height: meta.h,
297	      spawn: [meta.spawnX, meta.spawnY], groundLevel: meta.groundLevel, rockLevel: meta.rockLevel,
298	      lavaLine: meta.lavaLine,
299	      treeX: meta.treeX, treeStyle: meta.treeStyle, treeTops: meta.treeTops,
300	      crimson: meta.crimson, dungeonX: meta.dungeonX, dungeonY: meta.dungeonY, jungleX: meta.jungleX,
301	      created: Date.now(), playTimeMs,
302	      moonType: meta.moonType ?? 0,
303	      seedFlags: meta.seedFlags,
304	      difficulty: meta.difficulty ?? 0,   // Main.GameMode（旧档缺省=经典）
305	      journeyTimeRate: meta.journeyTimeRate ?? 1,
306	      journeyFreezeTime: meta.journeyFreezeTime ?? false,
307	      sundialCooldown: meta.sundialCooldown ?? 0,
308	      moondialCooldown: meta.moondialCooldown ?? 0,
309	      fastForwardTimeToDawn: meta.fastForwardTimeToDawn ?? false,
310	      fastForwardTimeToDusk: meta.fastForwardTimeToDusk ?? false,
311	    },
312	    clock: {
313	      timeOfDay: meta.timeOfDay, dayCount: meta.dayCount,
314	      bloodMoon: meta.bloodMoon ?? false, eclipse: meta.eclipse ?? false,
315	      moonPhase: meta.moonPhase ?? 0,
316	    },
317	    invasion: meta.invasion ?? { type: 0, size: 0, sizeStart: 0, x: 0, delay: 0 },
318	    altarCount: meta.altarCount ?? 0,
319	    meteorShowerCount: meta.meteorShowerCount ?? 0,
320	    critterChances: meta.critterChances ?? { fireFly: 30, fireFlyFriendly: 5, fireFlyMultiple: 15, butterfly: 10, stinkBug: 999999 },
321	    cultistDelay: meta.cultistDelay ?? 86400,   // 缺省 = delayStart（新世界 WF:1018 / v108 前旧档 WF:2188）
322	    angler: meta.angler ?? { quest: 0, whoFinishedToday: [] },   // WF:1329-1335（旧档缺省）
323	    savedOreTiers: meta.savedOreTiers ?? { cobalt: -1, mythril: -1, adamantite: -1 },
324	    shadowOrbCount: meta.shadowOrbCount ?? 0,
325	    tiles: bytesToB64(rleTiles(st, TILE_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL)),
326	    walls: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wall)),
327	    liquid: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquid)),
328	    liquidType: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquidType)),
329	    wire: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wire)),
330	    blocks: bytesToB64(rlePairs(combineBlocks(st.half, st.slope))),
331	    chests: meta.chests.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, locked: c.locked || undefined, items: c.items.map(mapItem) })),
332	    // v4:armor/dye/trash/miscEquips/miscDyes/loadouts 逐槽稳定 id(v3 是裸内部下标)
333	    player: {
334	      ...player,
335	      inventory: player.inventory.map(mapItem),
336	      banks: player.banks?.map((b) => b.map(mapItem)),
337	      armor: player.armor?.map(mapItem),
338	      dye: player.dye?.map(mapItem),
339	      trash: mapItem(player.trash ?? null),
340	      miscEquips: player.miscEquips?.map(mapItem),
341	      miscDyes: player.miscDyes?.map(mapItem),
342	      loadouts: player.loadouts?.map((l) => ({
343	        armor: l.armor.map(mapItem),
344	        dye: l.dye.map(mapItem),
345	      })),
346	    },
347	    npcs: townNpcs ?? [],   // 城镇 NPC 段(WorldFile.cs:1703-1750 NPC 段;曾硬编码 [] 断链)

352	      slots: e.slots.map((s) => (s ? { id: s.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[s.id] ?? 0, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix } : null)),
353	    })),
354	    flags: meta.flags,
355	    weather: meta.weather,
356	    events,
357	    bestiary: meta.bestiary,
358	    idScheme: 'sheet',
359	  };
360	  return JSON.stringify(data);
361	}
362	
363	/** half + slope → 单字节 blockType(原版 Tile.blockType: 0=整砖 1=半砖 2-5=slope1-4) */
364	function combineBlocks(half: Uint8Array, slope: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
365	  const out = new Uint8Array(half.length);
366	  for (let i = 0; i < half.length; i++) {
367	    out[i] = half[i] ? 1 : (slope[i] >= 1 && slope[i] <= 4 ? slope[i] + 1 : 0);
368	  }
369	  return out;
370	}
371

=== MapColors ===
// 生成期世界预览（GenWorldPreview / worker previewBitmap 共用）的 tile→RGB 逻辑。
// 返回 0xRRGGBB（字符串 hex 在热循环里太慢）。
// 注意：游戏内小地图（Renderer.colorFor/redrawAll LUT）是独立实现，未走本函数——
// 两份语义需保持一致（活性 tile > 液体 >32 > 墙 > 天空, MapHelper.cs GetWallType）。
import { TILE_DEFS, WALL_DEFS , TILE_BY_KEY } from '../data/tiles';
import { vanillaTileMapColor, vanillaWallMapColor, vanillaLiquidColor } from '../data/vanillaMapColors';
import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';

function hexToRgb24(hex: string | undefined, fallback: number): number {
  if (!hex || hex[0] !== '#') return fallback;
  const v = parseInt(hex.slice(1, 7), 16);
  return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : fallback;
}

// 预解析颜色表（id → 0xRRGGBB）
const TILE_COLORS = new Map<number, number>();
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  if (TILE_DEFS[i]) TILE_COLORS.set(i, hexToRgb24(TILE_DEFS[i].mapColor, 0x808080));
}
const WALL_COLORS = new Map<number, number>();
for (let i = 0; i < WALL_DEFS.length; i++) {
  if (WALL_DEFS[i]) WALL_COLORS.set(i, hexToRgb24(WALL_DEFS[i].mapColor, 0x2e2e2e));
}

const UNKNOWN = 0x808080;
const UNKNOWN_WALL = 0x2e2e2e;

// 原版地图色（MapHelper.cs 全表，tools/extract-mapcolors.mjs）：
// tile 按 vanilla.sheet / 墙按原版 id 直查；液体四色；背景天空渐变/土层/石层
const pack = (c: number[]): number => (c[0] << 16) | (c[1] << 8) | c[2];
const liqColor = (lt: number): number => pack(vanillaLiquidColor(lt >= 1 && lt <= 4 ? lt - 1 : 0));

/** tile → 0xRRGGBB。优先级：活性 tile > 液体 > 墙 > 背景（MapHelper 模型）。
 *  必须判 flags（=原版 tile.active()）：生成期 TileRunner 会在空气格写"幽灵 type"
 *  （原版 1:1 语义，数据层保留、finalize 净化）——不判 flags 会把幽灵画成实体，
 *  生成预览的天空就是被它们显形成"杂块"（游戏内渲染 ChunkCache 已判 flags） */
export function mapColor24(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): number {
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const t = st.flags[i] ? st.type[i] : 0;
  if (t !== 0) {
    // 自研像素方块（F2 图片导入）：RGB 编码在 frameX/Y（r<<8|g, b）——按原像素色显示
    const PIX = TILE_BY_KEY['pixel_block'];
    if (PIX !== undefined && t === PIX) {
      const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
      return ((fx >> 8) << 16) | ((fx & 255) << 8) | fy;
    }
    // 原版色优先（sheet 直查）；legacy def 回落 mapColor
    const sheet = TILE_DEFS[t]?.vanilla?.sheet;
    if (sheet !== undefined) {
      const vc = vanillaTileMapColor(sheet);
      if (vc) return pack(vc);
    }
    const c = TILE_COLORS.get(t);
    return c !== undefined ? c : UNKNOWN;
  }
  if (st.liquid[i] > 32) return liqColor(st.liquidType[i]);
  const w = st.wall[i];
  if (w !== 0) {
    const vc = vanillaWallMapColor(w);
    if (vc) return pack(vc);

Excellent. Vanilla NPC.cs:60971-61087 — the slime (type 1) color lerp. Key: paint consumption at :60973-60983:

```csharp
if (type == 1)
{
    bool flag = false;
    for (int j = (int)(base.Left.X / 16f); (float)j <= base.Right.X / 16f; j++)
    {
        int num10 = (int)((base.Bottom.Y + 1f) / 16f);
        if (WorldGen.SolidTile3(j, num10) && Main.tile[j, num10].color() > 0 && Main.tile[j, num10].color() != 30)
        {
            Color color = WorldGen.paintColor(Main.tile[j, num10].color());
            this.color = new Color((this.color.R * 19 + color.R) / 20, ..., (this.color.A * 19 + 100) / 20);
            flag = true;
        }
    }
    if (ai[1] > 0f && !flag) { ... item colors ... }
}
```

Key semantics:
- Scan tile columns from floor(Left.X/16) to floor(Right.X/16) inclusive
- num10 = floor((Bottom.Y + 1)/16) — the tile just below bottom
- Condition: SolidTile3(j, num10) && color>0 && color != 30 (NegativePaint excluded)
- Paint color lerp: each channel (c*19 + paintChannel)/20, alpha → (a*19+100)/20
- If painted tile found (flag=true), item colors are NOT applied.
- Note: the loop iterates multiple tiles; each iteration lerps, so stepping across two painted tiles lerps twice in one tick.

Also note the item color branch requires `ai[1] > 0f && !flag`.

Now WorldGen.paintColor — I need that. Let me grep WorldGen.cs for paintColor.
````

    let needWire = 0, needActuator = 0;
    const colorBits: Array<[number, number]> = [
      [TOOL_RED, TileStore.WIRE_RED], [TOOL_GREEN, TileStore.WIRE_GREEN],
      [TOOL_BLUE, TileStore.WIRE_BLUE], [TOOL_YELLOW, TileStore.WIRE_YELLOW],
    ];
    for (const [m, bit] of colorBits) if (mode & m && !(st.wire[st.idx(tx, ty)] & bit)) needWire++;
    if (mode & TOOL_ACTUATOR && !(st.wire[st.idx(tx, ty)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATOR)) needActuator++;
    const wireHave = inv.countOf(VI_ID(530));
    const actHave = inv.countOf(VI_ID(849));
    if (needWire > wireHave || needActuator > actHave) {
      this.cb.onToast(needActuator > actHave ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NoActuator') : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NoWire'));
      return;
    }
    let placed = false;
    for (const [m, bit] of colorBits) {
      if (mode & m && w.placeWire(tx, ty, bit)) placed = true;
    }
    if (mode & TOOL_ACTUATOR && w.placeActuator(tx, ty)) placed = true;
    if (placed) {
      if (needWire > 0) inv.remove(VI_ID(530), needWire);
      if (needActuator > 0) inv.remove(VI_ID(849), needActuator);
      this.player.useTime = 5;
      this.sfx.play('place');
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    }
    void dy;
  }

  /**
   * 铺设背景墙（Player.PlaceThing_Walls，Player.cs:38937-39071 + WorldGen.PlaceWall :75189 1:1）：
   *  ①射程内 && useTime 冷却 ②邻接门：四邻至少一格有实心 tile 或已有墙（防悬空墙）
   *  ③目标格无墙，或走 TileReplacement 替换（PlaceThing_TryReplacingWalls :39157-39166 →
   *    WorldGen.ReplaceWall :62562：NearFriendlyWall 门 + 可破坏墙门，成功直接换墙收货）
   *  ④PlaceWall 成功 → 消耗 + useTime
   *  ⑤FillEmptySpace（:38973）：stack>1 时，四邻中"空墙且其四邻全是本墙"的格自动补铺
   *  （消耗逐格扣）——原版铺墙手感"一笔涂一片"的核心
   */
  private tryPlaceWall(tx: number, ty: number, itemDef: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number]) {
    if (!this.netCanEditTile()) { this.netDeny(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.RoomProtectTiles')); return; }
    const st = this.world.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return;
    // 射程（ItemReach，与 tryPlace 一致 5.5 格）
    const dx = tx - Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE);
    const dy = ty - Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
    if (!this.inTileRange(tx, ty, (this.player.equipStats.tileRange ? 3 : 0) + (this.player.buffs.has(BuffType.Builder) ? 1 : 0))) return;
    if (this.player.useTime > 0) return;
    const wallId = itemDef.wallId!;
    const i0 = st.idx(tx, ty);
    if (st.wall[i0] === wallId) return;  // 已是同墙
    // 邻接门（cs:38943-38952）：四邻至少一格 active 或 wall>0
    const adj = (ax: number, ay: number) =>
      st.inBounds(ax, ay) && ((st.flags[st.idx(ax, ay)] && st.type[st.idx(ax, ay)] !== 0) || st.wall[st.idx(ax, ay)] > 0);
    if (!adj(tx + 1, ty) && !adj(tx - 1, ty) && !adj(tx, ty + 1) && !adj(tx, ty - 1)) return;
    // TileReplacement（Player.TileReplacementEnabled，cs:3225——builderAccStatus[10] 全程
    // 默认 0=恒开）：目标已有墙时先试替换（PlaceThing_TryReplacingWalls → ReplaceWall）。
    // 门 = NearFriendlyWall（WorldGen.cs:52290：自身或四邻墙为空/房墙）+ 墙可破坏
    // （KillWall_CheckFailure :60434：地牢墙需 downedBoss3 / 87 蜥蜴墙需 downedGolemBoss）
    // + 墙 ≠ 350。成功：旧墙尘 + 换墙 + 消耗（KillWall_DropItems 墙掉落表未移植——
    // 旧墙物品不掉，登记依赖）
    if (st.wall[i0] !== 0) {
      const oldWall = st.wall[i0];
      if (oldWall === 350) return;
      if (WALL_DUNGEON.has(oldWall) && !this.world.flags.downedSkeletron) return;
      if (oldWall === 87 && !this.world.flags['downed_245']) return;
      // NearFriendlyWall：自身/四邻任一格墙为空或房墙（WALL_HOUSE 表）
      const friendly = (ax: number, ay: number) =>
        !st.inBounds(ax, ay) || st.wall[st.idx(ax, ay)] === 0 || WALL_HOUSE.has(st.wall[st.idx(ax, ay)]);
      if (!(friendly(tx, ty) || friendly(tx - 1, ty) || friendly(tx + 1, ty)
        || friendly(tx, ty - 1) || friendly(tx, ty + 1))) return;
      st.setWall(tx, ty, wallId);
      this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, '#5C4436', 6, 1.5); // KillWall_MakeWallDust×10 近似
      this.sfx.play('chop', 1, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8);               // KillWall_PlaySounds
      this.player.inv.removeAt(this.player.inv.selected, 1);
      this.player.useTime = Math.max(4, Math.round(15 * ((this.player.equipStats.wallSpeed || this.player.buffs.has(BuffType.Builder)) ? 0.75 : 1)));
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
      return;
    }
    // WorldGen.PlaceWall（:75189-75205）：仅 wall==0 可放；成功才继续
    st.setWall(tx, ty, wallId);
    this.sfx.play('dig', 1, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8);
    this.player.inv.removeAt(this.player.inv.selected, 1);
    // wallSpeed=1（Player.cs:2336），铺墙节奏 ≈ 常规放置；便携水泥机族 WallSpeedAcc 冷却 -25%
    this.player.useTime = Math.max(4, Math.round(15 * ((this.player.equipStats.wallSpeed || this.player.buffs.has(BuffType.Builder)) ? 0.75 : 1)));
    this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    // FillEmptySpace（cs:38973-39025）：stack>1 时补铺被本墙四面包围的空洞
    if (this.player.inv.heldItem()?.stack ?? 0 > 1) {
      for (const [ox, oy] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]] as const) {
        const nx = tx + ox, ny = ty + oy;
        if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny) || st.wall[st.idx(nx, ny)] !== 0) continue;
        let surround = 0;
        for (const [qx, qy] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]] as const) {
          const qx2 = nx + qx, qy2 = ny + qy;
          if (st.inBounds(qx2, qy2) && st.wall[st.idx(qx2, qy2)] === wallId) surround++;
        }
        if (surround !== 4) continue;
        st.setWall(nx, ny, wallId);
        this.player.inv.removeAt(this.player.inv.selected, 1);
        this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
      }
    }
  }

// 物品注册表
export type ToolType = 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer';
// Buff 类型（药水饮用后生效）
export type PotionBuff = 'agility' | 'ironskin' | 'resistance' | 'thorns' | 'regen'
  | 'obsidianSkin' | 'gills' | 'manaRegen' | 'magicPower' | 'featherfall' | 'waterWalking' | 'archery' | 'nightOwl';
// 盔甲部位：0 头盔 1 胸甲 2 胫甲（与 Inventory.armorSlotReq 的格位对应）
export type ArmorSlot = 0 | 1 | 2;

export interface ItemDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  maxStack: number;
  tile?: string;          // 放置的 tile key
  placeStyle?: number;     // 放置的样式索引(vanilla style 帧 X 偏移 = style*fw*18)
  magic?: {               // 魔法武器:消耗 mana 发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    manaCost: number;
    projSpeed: number;
    proj?: 'spark' | 'bolt'; // 投射物外观
  };
  ranged?: {              // 远程武器:消耗 ammo 弹药发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    knockback: number;
    ammo: string;         // 弹药 item key(箭)
    projSpeed: number;    // 投射物速度 px/tick
    /** 原版 item id（PickAmmo/ItemCheck_Shoot 数值以 vanilla-itemcombat.json 为准） */
    vid?: number;
  };
  /** 原版 AmmoID（40=箭；弹药物品自身标识，PickAmmo 匹配弓 useAmmo） */
  ammoId?: number;
  /** 原版 item id（战斗数值数据源） */
  vid?: number;
  wall?: string;          // 放置的 wall key
  /** 原版 createWall（Item.cs SetDefaults）：放置的背景墙 vanilla id。
   *  vanilla-wallitems.json 全量 124 项（tools/extract-wallitems.mjs 提取） */
  wallId?: number;
  tool?: {
    type: ToolType;
    power?: number;       // 工具力（镐/斧/锤）
    damage?: number;      // 武器伤害
    speed?: number;       // 挥砍动画 tick（≈原版 useAnimation）
    /** 原版 useTime（Item.cs SetDefaults）：挖掘每击冷却——原版挖掘节奏的真基准，
     *  铜 15-23 / 铁 13-20 / 银 11-19 / 金 17-23（applyItemTime 冷却=useTime） */
    useTime?: number;
    knockback?: number;
    reach?: number;       // 攻击/作用半径（px）
  };
  /** 副工具力（Hamaxe 系双工具：主 tool.type 外的另一力，挖掘入口按主类型判定） */
  axePower?: number;
  heal?: number;          // 食用/饮用回复
  potion?: {
    buff: PotionBuff;
    duration: number;     // 秒
    isHealType: boolean;  // 受"耐药性"封锁
  };
  accessory?: 'lucky_horseshoe' | 'feral_claws'; // 配饰效果
  armor?: { slot: ArmorSlot; defense: number };   // 盔甲（可穿装备/时装格，仅装备格计防御）
  value?: number;         // 钱币价值（铜币）
  wireTool?: {            // 电路工具（原版 Player.cs:30289-30444 ItemCheck_UseWiringTools）
    place?: number;       // 单击放置的导线位掩码(TOOL_RED/BLUE/GREEN/YELLOW/ACTUATOR)
    cutter?: boolean;     // 剪线钳:按优先级移除一件(致动器>黄>绿>蓝>红)
    rod?: boolean;        // 致动魔杖:手动翻转致动状态
    grand?: boolean;      // 宏伟蓝图:拖拽批量(R 键切模式)
  };
  desc?: string;
}

export const ITEM_DEFS: ItemDef[] = [];
const byKey: Record<string, number> = {};
export const ITEM_BY_KEY: Record<string, number> = byKey;

function item(d: Partial<ItemDef> & { key: string; name: string }) {
  byKey[d.key] = ITEM_DEFS.length;
  ITEM_DEFS.push({ maxStack: 999, ...d } as ItemDef);
}

// ---- 基础方块 ----

```

360	/** 物品原版 id（def.vid 优先，vi_NNNN_ key 前缀兜底） */
361	export function vanillaItemIdOf(def: ItemDef | null | undefined): number | null {
362	  if (!def) return null;
363	  if (def.vid != null) return def.vid;
364	  const m = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(def.key);
365	  return m ? Number(m[1]) : null;

368	interface HeldSemantics {
369	  vid: number | null;

376	function heldSemantics(held: SmartCursorHeld): HeldSemantics {
377	  const def = held.def;

488	      () => this.stepStaffOfRegrowth(env, sem, reach, (x, y) => { fX = x; fY = y; }, () => fX !== -1),
489	    );
490	    for (const step of steps) {
491	      if (fX !== -1 && fY !== -1) break; // 首个命中独占（:118-156 顺序执行）
492	      step();
493	    }

496	      this.y = fY;
497	      this.showing = true;

550	    if (y < Math.max(1, this.world.rockLevel)) return hex(VANILLA_DIRT_BG);
551	    return hex(VANILLA_ROCK_BG);

554	  redrawAll() {

558	    const { world, ctx } = this;
559	    const st = world.store;
560	    if (!this._mapLUT || this._mapLUT.tiles.length < TILE_DEFS.length) {

563	        return 0xff000000 | ((v & 255) << 16) | (v & 0xff00) | ((v >> 16) & 255); // 小端 ABGR
564	      };
565	      const rgb = (c: number[]): number => 0xff000000 | ((c[2] & 255) << 16) | ((c[1] & 255) << 8) | (c[0] & 255); // 小端：bits16=B（同 parse）

599	    const surf = Math.max(1, world.groundLevel), rock = Math.max(surf + 1, world.rockLevel);
600	    for (let y = 0; y < world.h; y++) {

602	      const skyC = vanillaSkyColor(y, surf);
603	      const bg = y < surf ? (0xff000000 | ((skyC[2] & 255) << 16) | ((skyC[1] & 255) << 8) | (skyC[0] & 255))
604	        : y < rock ? lut.dirtBg : lut.rockBg;
605	      for (let x = 0; x < world.w; x++) {

649	      }
650	    }
651	  }

// 原版物品 id → 注册 key/内部 id 的统一查表入口(本地物品退役后全库唯一物品字面量源)。
// ★ 严禁手拼 vi_ 键字符串:蛇形/驼峰双注册下唯一权威是 VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID(驼峰)。
// 后续 dual-key 清理(蛇形/驼峰归一)只需改本文件,消费点无感。
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID, ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';

/** 原版物品 id → 注册 key(查不到返回 '') */
export function VI(vid: number): string {
  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? '';
}

/** 原版物品 id → 内部 id(查不到返回 0) */
export function VI_ID(vid: number): number {
  const k = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
  return k !== undefined ? (ITEM_BY_KEY[k] ?? 0) : 0;
}

  TABLE: VI(32), CHAIR: VI(34), BED: VI(224), TOMBSTONE: VI(321), LIFE_CRYSTAL: VI(29),

break;      // ShadowPaint\n    case 30: r = 200; g = 200; b = 200; a = 150; break; // NegativePaint（半透明）\n    default: break;                              // 0/31 → White（C# 初值）\n  }\n  return [r, g, b, a];\n}\n\n/** 涂层 id → RGBA（WorldGen.cs:44063-44071 coatingColor）。1=荧光 2=隐形，其余透明。\n *  涂层系统未建（见文件头），仅涂刷尘/UI 可能消费 */\nexport function coatingColor(coating: number): readonly [number, number, number, number] {\n  switch (coating) {\n    case 1: return [235, 170, 255, 255];\n    case 2: return [180, 245, 255, 255];\n    default: return [0, 0, 0, 0]; // Color.Transparent\n  }\n}\n\n// shader，此处用\n * 等价逐像素公式。原版 shader 语义（Terraria.GameContent/TilePaintSystemV2.cs，\n * shader \"TileShader\" 的 PaintColorData）：\n *   - 普通漆(1-12)：逐通道 c' = c * p / 255（乘色）\n *   - 深层漆(13-24)：与普通同 RGB，但替换为“覆盖式”混合 c' = c*0.5 + p*0.5\n *     （深层漆覆盖更强、能压住原色，官方美术语义“Deep paint 遮盖力更强”）\n *   - 黑漆 25 / 暗影 29：向黑收敛 c' = c * p / 255（黑漆 p=75/255、暗影 p=25/255）\n *   - 白漆 26：不调制（恒 White，乘法等于原色）\n *   - 负相 30：负片 c' = 255 - c（alpha 150 不参与 tile 乘色）\n *\n * ★ 等价边界（登记）：原版深层漆真实公式在 shader 常量表（PaintData）里，\n *   本仓按上述覆盖混合实现；普通漆/黑漆/白漆/负相为精确等价。\n */\nexport function applyPaintTint(\n  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number,\n): readonly [number, number, number] {\n  if (paint <= 0) return [r, g, b];\n  if (paint === 26) return [r, g, b];                 // 白漆：乘 255 = 原色\n  if (paint === 30) return [255 - r, 255 - g, 255 - b]; // 负相：反转\n  const [pr, pg, pb] = paintColor(paint);\n  if (paint >= 13 && paint <= 24) {\n    // 深层漆：覆盖式混合（见上）\n    return [(r * 128 + pr * 128) >> 8, (g * 128 + pg * 128) >> 8, (b * 128 + pb * 128) >> 8];\n  }\n  // 普通漆 1-12 / 黑 25 / 灰 27 / 棕 28 / 暗影 29：逐通道乘色\n  return [(r * pr) >> 8, (g * pg) >> 8, (b * pb) >> 8];\n}\n\n/** paint id → css 颜色（小地图 LUT / 调试用） */\nexport function paintCss(paint: number): string {\n  const [r, g, b] = paintColor(paint);\n  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n}\n"

````
MapHelper.MapColor (1812-1863) — EXACT C# minimap paint formula! This is what I need for the Minimap LUT:

```csharp
private static void MapColor(ushort type, ref Color oldColor, byte colorType)
{
    Color color = WorldGen.paintColor(colorType);
    float num = oldColor.R / 255f, num2 = oldColor.G/255f, num3 = oldColor.B/255f;
    // sort so num = max(num, num2, num3), num2 = second, num3 = min
    if (num2 > num) { swap(num, num2) }
    if (num3 > num) { swap(num, num3) }
    switch (colorType)
    {
    case 29: // ShadowPaint
        float num7 = num3 * 0.3f;   // note: num3 = MIN channel
        oldColor.R = color.R * num7; G = color.G * num7; B = color.B * num7;
        break;
    case 30: // Negative
        if (type >= wallRangeStart && type <= wallRangeEnd) {
            oldColor.R = (255 - oldColor.R) * 0.5f; ...
        } else {
            oldColor.R = 255 - oldColor.R; ...
        }
        break;
    default:
        float num6 = num;  // MAX channel
        oldColor.R = color.R * num6; G = color.G * num6; B = color.B * num6;
        break;
    }
}
```

So the minimap paint: scale paint color by the tile's MAX channel (luminance-preserving hue replacement!). That's the "which tile 不可涂/覆盖" answer: deep paints use the same formula on the minimap (default case) — deep paint on minimap = same as normal.

Also for negative paint on walls: (255-c)*0.5.

And GetMapTileXnaColor (1865-1882): oldColor = colorLookup[type]; if Color > 0 → MapColor; then multiply by light.

MapTile.Color comes from GetTileType: `newColor = tileCache.color()` (line 1939/1971) and GetWallType: `newColor = tileCache.wallColor()` (1992). Let me read GetTileType/GetWallType around 1914-2010 to get exact semantics.

158	/** 直接从内存 SaveData 构建(导入 .wld 路径用——跳过 stringify→parse 双拷贝,
159	 *  2026-08 审计 #3:该往返单次 20~50MB×2) */
160	export function loadSaveData(data: SaveData, onTilesProgress?: (p: number) => void): { world: World; player: SaveData['player']; data: SaveData } {
161	  // 物品 id 还原(箱子/背包/储物就地重写):v3+ 走稳定 id;v2 旧档直读(接受破损政策)。
162	  // v4 起 armor/dye/trash/misc/loadouts 也是稳定 id;v3 恰为裸内部下标——用迁移锚
163	  // 快照(LEGACY_KEY_OF_INTERNAL_V3)反查 key 再转 vi_/原定义(本地物品退役兼容层)
164	  const v3 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 3;
165	  const v4 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 4;
166	  const mapItem = (it: SavedItemT | null): SavedItemT | null =>
167	    it && v3 ? { ...it, id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE.get(it.id) ?? 0 } : it;
168	  const mapLegacySlot = (it: SavedItemT | null): SavedItemT | null => {
169	    if (!it) return it;
170	    if (v4) return mapItem(it);
171	    if (!v3) return it; // v2 及更早:裸下标直读
172	    // v3:armor/dye/trash/misc/loadouts 段 = 保存时构建的裸内部下标
173	    const key = LEGACY_KEY_OF_INTERNAL_V3[it.id];
174	    if (key === undefined) return it;
175	    if (/^vi_(\d+)_/.test(key)) {
176	      // vi_ 键:键本身仍在注册表(蛇形/驼峰双注册内容等价),直接反查内部 id。
177	      // ★ 不能经 ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[裸下标] —— 本地 184 条删除后下标整体左移
178	      const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[key];
179	      return id !== undefined ? { ...it, id } : it;
180	    }
181	    // 本地键:退役映射(→vi_);纯自创删除键→归 0
182	    const viId = viIdOfRetired(key);
183	    return { ...it, id: viId > 0 ? viId : 0 };
184	  };

/** FindPaintOrCoating（Player.cs:41272-41289）：弹药栏 54-57 优先，再全包 0-57。

findPaintSlot(): number {

}

}

    this.slots = Array(INV_SIZE).fill(null);
    this.armor = Array(ARMOR_TOTAL).fill(null);
    this.dye = Array(DYE_SLOTS).fill(null);
    this.miscEquips = Array(MISC_SLOTS).fill(null);
    this.miscDyes = Array(MISC_SLOTS).fill(null);
    this.loadouts = Array.from({ length: LOADOUT_COUNT }, () => ({
      armor: Array(ARMOR_TOTAL).fill(null),
      dye: Array(DYE_SLOTS).fill(null),
    }));
  }

  /** 切换 Loadout（Player.TrySwitchingLoadout :5430-5434：先 Swap 当前套装备份仓、
   *  再 Swap 目标仓——玩家身上即换上目标套装，原套装入当前备份仓） */
  trySwitchingLoadout(idx: number): void {
    if (idx === this.currentLoadout || idx < 0 || idx >= LOADOUT_COUNT) return;
    const swap = (a: Array<InvItem | null>, b: Array<InvItem | null>, n: number) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const t = a[i]; a[i] = b[i]; b[i] = t; }
    };
    const cur = this.loadouts[this.currentLoadout];
    const next = this.loadouts[idx];
    swap(this.armor, cur.armor, ARMOR_TOTAL);
    swap(this.dye, cur.dye, DYE_SLOTS);
    swap(this.armor, next.armor, ARMOR_TOTAL);
    swap(this.dye, next.dye, DYE_SLOTS);
    this.currentLoadout = idx;
  }

  heldItem(): InvItem | null {
    return this.slots[this.selected];
  }

```

3 : 0)\n      + (this.player.equipStats.tileRange ? 3 : 0)\n      + (this.player.buffs.has(BuffType.Builder) ? 1 : 0);\n    if (!this.inTileRange(tx, ty, tb)) return;\n    const p = this.player;\n    const i0 = st.idx(tx, ty);\n    const paintDust: PaintDustHook = (x, y, np, op) => {\n      // paintEffect（WorldGen.cs:44091-44112）：10 颗尘，半数 noGravity×1.2、半数×0.5\n      const [r, g, b] = paintDustColor(np, op);\n      const col = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;\n      for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) {\n        this.spawnParticles(x * TILE + Math.random() * TILE, y * TILE + Math.random() * TILE,\n          col, 1, k % 2 === 0 ? 1.2 : 0.5, { life: 40, size: 1 });\n      }\n    };\n    const useAnimation = 15; // Item.cs useAnimation 15（三件套与 Spectre 同值）\n    const useTimeBase = 10;  // Item.cs useTime 10\n    // ApplyItemTime(sItem, tileSpeed/wallSpeed)（Player.cs:4221-4230）：useTime×乘子，下限 1\n    const applyItemTime = (multiplier: number) => {\n      const eq = this.player.equipStats;\n      const m = (eq.tileRange != null || eq.wallSpeed ? multiplier : multiplier);\n      void m;\n      p.useTime = Math.max(1, Math.round(useTimeBase * multiplier));\n      this.swing = { t: useAnimation, dur: useAnimation, item: p.inv.heldItem()?.id ?? 0 };\n      this.swingHitSet.clear();\n      this.swingTileCutSet.clear();\n    };\n\n    if (itemDef.paintTool === 'brush') {\n      // :41262 目标格必须 active\n      if (!(st.flags[i0] && st.type[i0] !== 0)) return;\n      if (p.useTime > 0) return; // :41265 ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation>0 && controlUseItem\n      applyItemTime(this.paintTileSpeed());\n      this.tryPainting(tx, ty, false, paintDust);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (itemDef.paintTool === 'roller') {\n      // :41244 目标格必须有墙\n      if (st.wall[i0] === 0) return;\n      if (p.useTime > 0) return;\n      applyItemTime(this.paintWallSpeed());\n      this.tryPainting(tx, ty, true, paintDust);\n      return;\n    }\n    // scraper\n    // TryScrapping :41218——两通道均无漆 → 直接返回（不挥动不耗冷却）\n    const hasPaint = (st.wall[i0] > 0 && st.paintWall[i0] > 0)\n      || ((st.flags[i0] && st.type[i0] !== 0) && st.paint[i0] > 0);\n    if (hasPaint) {\n      if (p.useTime > 0) return;\n      // :41225-41232 先 tile 后 wall；成功才 ApplyItemTime（tileSpeed/wallSpeed 各自）\n      if (paintTile(st, tx, ty, 0, paintDust)) {\n        applyItemTime(this.paintTileSpeed());\n      } else if (paintWall(st, tx, ty, 0, paintDust)) {\n        applyItemTime(this.paintWallSpeed());\n      } else {\n        p.useTime = 0; // 双原语都未改色（理论不可达）——不耗冷却\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n    // LongMoss :41166-41213：tile 184 → KillTile + 1/9 苔藓掉落\n    if (st.type[i0] === (TILE_BY_KEY['v_184_moss_plants'] ?? -1) && st.flags[i0]) {\n      if (p.useTime > 0) return;\n      const frameX = st.frameX[i0];\n      if (!this.tryMineTileDirect(tx, ty)) return; // WorldGen.KillTile :41179\n      applyItemTime(1);                            // ApplyItemTime(sItem)（:41184，无乘子）\n      // :41189-41212 1/9 掉苔藓：4349+frameX/22，6-10 档特例\n      if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 9) === 0) {\n        const col = Math.floor(frameX / 22);\n        let mossVid = 4349 + col;\n        switch (col) {\n          case 6: mossVid = 4377; break;\n          case 7: mossVid = 4378; break;\n          case 8: mossVid = 4389; break;\n          case 9: mossVid = 5127; break;\n          case 10: mossVid = 5128; break;\n        }\n        const mossKey = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[mossVid];\n        const mossId = mossKey !== undefined ?   breakTile(x: number, y: number, byPlayer = false) {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const type = st.get(x, y);
    if (type === 0) return;
    if (byPlayer) {
      const sheet = TILE_DEFS[type]?.vanilla?.sheet;
      if (sheet != null) this.achievements.notifyTileDestroyed(sheet);
      this.achievements.handleMining();
    }
    const def = TILE_DEFS[type];

    // 倒木（KillTile type 488 → mysticLogsEvent.FallenLogDestroyed，WorldGen.cs:49866）：
    // 仙女事件桩表重扫（桩被拆当夜不再出仙女；StartNight 亦会重扫）
    if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_488_fallen_log']) {
      mysticLogs.fallenLogDestroyed(this.fairyCtx());
    }

    // 树：砍树干 → 整棵树倒下（自有树 + 导入的原版树族）
    if (type === T.TREE) {
      this.fellTree(x, y);
      return;
    }
    // 暗影之球/恶魔之心：整体破碎（原版 WorldGen.CheckOrb 移植）
    if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_31_orb_heart']) {
      this.smashOrbHeart(x, y);
      return;
    }
    // 瓦罐：统一走 smashPot（整罐洪水填充 + loot）。
    // 不能落入下方 object 锚点分支——瓦罐帧编码是【底行 frameY=0、顶行 frameY=18】
    // （贴图表视觉 Y 偏移，与家具的"顶行 0"相反），锚点公式会按 gy 上移 →
    // 清掉罐底下一格的地面、罐体留在原地 → 可重复破坏刷战利品
    if (type === POT_TILE) {
      this.smashPot(x, y);
      return;
    }
    if (IMPORTED_TREE_TYPES.has(type)) {
      this.fellImportedTree(x, y, type);
      return;
    }
    // 幼虫（原版 KillTile case 231, WorldGen.cs:53254-53280）：整件破碎无掉落，
    // 300 格内召蜂后——须在 object 锚点分支前拦（否则按家具掉 place_ 物品）
    if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_231_larva']) {
      this.breakLarva(x, y);
      return;
    }
    // 蜂巢块（原版 KillTile case 225, WorldGen.cs:65524-65545）：1/3 变满蜂蜜
    // （打破流蜜），否则掉蜂巢物品 + 1/2 概率出 1-2 只蜜蜂
    if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_225_hive_block']) {
      this.breakHive(x, y);
      return;
    }

```

So my changes introduce ZERO new tsc errors. 

Interaction layer done except verifying SmartCursor tests pass. Now task #97: rendering consumption.

1. ChunkCache tile draw by paint multiply: In renderChunk, when st.paint[i] > 0, apply tint. The complication: ChunkCache draws whole sprites via drawImage. To tint, I can set a filter or use globalCompositeOperation. The cleanest for canvas: pre-tint via a temp canvas per paint id? Per-tile: draw the cell to a scratch canvas, then multiply. That's expensive per tile.

Better approach: since paint is per-tile and chunks are baked, I can:
- Build a tinted copy of the tile sheet per (sheet, paint) — too many combos.
- Or: draw the tile normally to the chunk, then apply a multiply pass over that 16x16 region using globalCompositeOperation='multiply' with the paint color — this multiplies EVERYTHING in that rect including transparency... multiply on transparent pixels: canvas 'multiply' blend composites source over dest with blending; dest transparent areas become the source color? With 'multiply', result = source*dest blended with alpha compositing. If dest is transparent (alpha 0), result = source color at source alpha. That would colorize empty space — wrong.

Standard technique for tinting sprites in canvas 2D:
```
ctx.save();
ctx.drawImage(img, ...); // draw sprite

 "new_string": "    ctx.globalAlpha = 1; // 致动幽灵 alpha 复位(防影响后续 pass)\n\n    // ---- 油漆乘色 pass：墙层 + tile 层分别着色 ----\n    // 原版 tile/wall 各自经 tileShader 按格取 paint（TilePaintSystemV2）；\n    // Canvas 等价实现见 tintRegion（乘色模型见 world/Paint.applyPaintTint 注释）。\n    // ★ 等价边界：原版按\"绘制调用\"着色（多格物件整张贴图随锚格上色）；\n    //   本实现按 16×16 格区域着色——涂多格家具/树只有被涂格区域显色（登记）\n    for (let ly = 0; ly < CHUNK; ly++) {\n      for (let lx = 0; lx < CHUNK; lx++) {\n        const x = x0 + lx, y = y0 + ly;\n        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;\n        const i = st.idx(x, y);\n        const pw = st.paintWall[i];\n        if (pw > 0 && st.wall[i] !== 0) tintRegion(wctx, wall, lx * TILE, ly * TILE, pw);\n        const pt = st.paint[i];\n        if (pt > 0 && st.flags[i] && st.type[i] !== 0) tintRegion(ctx, tile, lx * TILE, ly * TILE, pt);\n      }\n    }\n"
}

````
Now the minimap logic. Vanilla (MapHelper):
- GetTileType (:1914-1973): tile active → newColor = tile.color(), EXCEPT num==160 → newColor=0. Also invisibleBlock → baseType=0 (coating, N/A).
- GetWallType (:1976-2014): wall>0 (not liquid) → newColor = tile.wallColor(), EXCEPT walls 21/88-93/168/241 → 0.
- GetMapTileXnaColor (:1865-1882): base = colorLookup[mapType]; if Color>0 → MapColor(mapType, ref, color); then light multiply (our map has no light channel → skip).

Also note CreateMapTile priority: tile → wall → background. Our colorFor/redrawAll already follow that.

So in our minimap:
1. colorFor(x,y): after computing the tile color (vanillaTileMapColor or mapColor), if st.paint[i] > 0 AND sheet !== 160 → apply mapPaintColor(false, rgb, paint). For walls: if st.paintWall[i] > 0 AND wall not in {21,88..93,168,241} → mapPaintColor(true, rgb, paint).

2. redrawAll loop: same, working on packed ABGR. I need to unpack → mapPaintColor → repack. Write a small helper inside Minimap:

```ts
/** MapHelper.MapColor（:1812-1863）的 ABGR 打包版：油漆换色 */
private applyMapPaint(packed: number, paint: number, isWall: boolean): number {
  const r = packed & 255, g = (packed >> 8) & 255, b = (packed >> 16) & 255;
  const [nr, ng, nb] = mapPaintColor(isWall, [r, g, b], paint);
  return 0xff000000 | ((nb & 255) << 16) | ((ng & 255) << 8) | (nr & 255);
}
```
Wait: the LUT packs little-endian ABGR: bits0-7 = R, 8-15 = G, 16-23 = B (from `rgb()` helper: `(c[2]&255)<<16 | (c[1]&255)<<8 | (c[0]&255)`). So packed & 255 = R. Yes as written.

Note mapPaintColor uses (color * num6) | 0 with C# byte cast = truncation toward zero — same as |0 for positives. C# `(byte)(float)` cast truncates. Good.

But careful: C# oldColor.R is a byte and `(float)(int)oldColor.R / 255f` then multiplied. My mapPaintColor takes rgb[0..2] as the base color bytes — correct.

Also MapColor default branch scales by MAX channel: num = max(r,g,b)/255. If a tile's map color is dark, paint barely shows — that's vanilla behavior (paint replaces hue keeping luminance). 

Now the map-ignore lists:
- Tiles: sheet 160 (MapHelper.cs:1965-1968). 
- Walls: 21, 88, 89, 90, 91, 92, 93, 168, 241 (:1993-2005).

Implement as module consts in Renderer near Minimap, or reuse from Paint.ts? They're MapHelper semantics — put them in Renderer (minimap consumer) as consts with line refs. Actually cleaner in Paint.ts since it's the paint module... but they're map-domain. I'll put in Renderer as exported consts for tests.

Let me write the edits: colorFor + redrawAll + flushDirty (flushDirty uses colorFor → covered).

For colorFor, the tile branch returns hex strings; apply paint before returning.
````

mapPaintColor expects `readonly [number, number, number]`. Passing a plain array literal `[a,b,c]` to that parameter works (TS allows array literal → tuple). But rgb: readonly number[] can't be cast directly to tuple. So pass `[rgb[0], rgb[1], rgb[2]]` literal — TS infers number[] which is not assignable to a tuple... Actually TS will contextually type the array literal as the tuple when the parameter type is a tuple — yes, contextual typing works for array literals assigned to tuple parameter types. So `mapPaintColor(false, [rgb[0], rgb[1], rgb[2]], p)` should type-check.

{
 "replace_all": false,

 "old_string": "  private slimeColorTick() {\n    if (this.vanillaId !== 1 || this.ai1 <= 0 || !this.colorRGBA) return;\n    const t = Enemy.SLIME_ITEM_COLORS[this.ai1];\n    if (!t) return;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) this.colorRGBA[k] = (this.colorRGBA[k] * 19 + t[k]) / 20;\n    this.colorRGBA[3] = (this.colorRGBA[3] * 19 + t[3]) / 20;\n  }",
 "new_string": "  private slimeColorTick(game: GameHooks) {\n    if (this.vanillaId !== 1 || !this.colorRGBA) return;\n    // ---- 踩油漆扫描（NPC.cs:60973-60983）----\n    // j 自 floor(Left.X/16) 至 floor(Right.X/16)（含端点，跨格史莱姆每格各 lerp一次）；\n    // num10 = floor((Bottom.Y+1)/16)（底边下一格）；门槛 = SolidTile3（:70470-70489\n    // active && !inActive && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop）&& color>0 && color!=30（负相不吃）\n    const st = game.world.store;\n    let flag = false;\n    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h + 1) / TILE);\n    for (let j = Math.floor(this.x / TILE); j <= Math.floor((this.x + this.w) / TILE); j++) {\n      if (!st.inBounds(j, ty)) continue;\n      const i = st.idx(j, ty);\n      const t = st.type[i];\n      // SolidTile3：非活性格/已致动/非实心/平台（tileSolidTop）都不算立足点\n      if (!(st.flags[i] && t !== 0)) continue;\n      if (st.wire[i] & 32) continue;                       // inActive（致动缩入）\n      const d = TILE_DEFS[t];\n      if (!d?.solid || d.platform) continue;\n      const pc = st.paint[i];\n      if (pc > 0 && pc !== 30) {\n        const c = paintColor(pc);                          // WorldGen.paintColor :60979\n        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) this.colorRGBA[k] = (this.colorRGBA[k] * 19 + c[k]) / 20;\n        this.colorRGBA[3] = (this.colorRGBA[3] * 19 + 100) / 20; // :60980 alpha 目标恒 100\n        flag = true;\n      }\n    }\n    // ---- 体内物品色（NPC.cs:60984-61086：ai[1]>0 且本轮无踩漆才生效） ----\n    if (this.ai1 <= 0 || flag) return;\n    const t = Enemy.SLIME_ITEM_COLORS[this.ai1];\n    if (!t) return;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) this.colorRGBA[k] = (this.colorRGBA[k] * 19 + t[k]) / 20;\n    this.colorRGBA[3] = (this.colorRGBA[3] * 19 + t[3]) / 20;\n  }"
}

3. paintWall: wall==0 → false; same → false; diff → true.
4. TileStore: setPaint/setPaintWall write + listeners fired (paint change → listener).
5. KillTile/PlaceTile paint clearing: setTile(0) clears paint; setTile on inactive clears; setTile replacing active tile keeps paint.
6. setWall(0) clears paintWall.
7. Save roundtrip: serializeSave → loadSaveData → paint preserved (use small world).
8. Old save (no paint field) → all zeros.
9. mapPaintColor 1:1: default branch scales by max channel; shadow 29 uses median channel ×0.3; negative 30 wall vs tile.
10. applyPaintTint: multiply for paint 1; identity for 26; invert for 30.
11. Item paint table: ITEM_DEFS paint for vid 1073 (RedPaint) → 1; 1099 → 27; 1968 → 30; paintTool for 1071/1072/1100/1543-1545.
12. Inventory.findPaintSlot: ammo slot priority (54-57 first), then 0-57; heldPaintLookup.
13. SmartCursor three strategies: brush picks nearest active unpainted tile; requires paintLookup; roller requires wall & no solid in front; scraper picks painted tile or moss 184.

For SmartCursor tests, look at tests/smart-cursor.test.ts to reuse its env-building harness.

Let me look at the existing smart-cursor test to model mine.

} from '../src/player/SmartCursor';

const W = 220, H = 160;
const PT = 100, PYT = 95;              // 玩家所在格（世界中央）
const px = PT * 16, py = PYT * 16;     // position（px）

function mkActor() {
  return { x: px, y: py, w: 20, h: 42, cx: px + 10, cy: py + 21, facing: 1 };
}

function mkEnv(
  st: TileStore, mouse: { x: number; y: number },
  held: { def: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number] | null; tool: { type: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer' | 'sword'; power: number } | null },
  over: Partial<SmartCursorEnv> = {},
): SmartCursorEnv {
  return {
    st,
    mouse,
    actor: mkActor(),
    held,
    settings: { ...DEFAULT_SMART_CURSOR_SETTINGS },
    tileBoost: 0,
    wireToolMode: 0,
    occupants: [],
    controlUseItem: true,
    ...over,
  };
}

/** 跑一次 lookup 并返回目标（无目标 null） */
function run(env: SmartCursorEnv, sc = new SmartCursor()): { x: number; y: number } | null {

  sc.lookup(env);

// MapColors（生成期预览的 tile→色公共逻辑）确定性测试
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { mapColor24 } from '../src/render/MapColors';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { T, TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';

function hex(c: number): string {
  return '#' + c.toString(16).padStart(6, '0').toUpperCase();
}

describe('mapColor24', () => {
  it('优先级：实体 tile > 液体 > 墙 > 天空', () => {
    const w = new World(4, 4, 1, 't');
    const st = w.store;
    const x = 1, y = 1;
    // 天空
    expect(hex(mapColor24(st, x, y))).toBe('#91B9FF'); // 生成期天空兜底（原版渐变浅段近似）
    // 墙
    st.setWallSilent(x, y, 1);
    expect(hex(mapColor24(st, x, y))).not.toBe('#91B9FF'); // 原版墙1=(52,52,52)
    // 液体覆盖墙
    st.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 1);
    expect(hex(mapColor24(st, x, y))).toBe('#093DBF'); // 原版水 array3=(9,61,191)
    // 岩浆
    st.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 2);
    expect(hex(mapColor24(st, x, y))).toBe('#FD2003'); // 原版岩浆 array3=(253,32,3)
    // 实体 tile 覆盖液体
    st.setTileSilent(x, y, T.STONE);
    expect(hex(mapColor24(st, x, y))).toBe('#808080'); // 原版石 sheet1=(128,128,128)
  });

  it('液体需 >32 才显示（原版 Minimap 阈值,MapHelper.cs:1983）', () => {
    const w = new World(2, 2, 1, 't');
    w.store.setLiquid(0, 0, 20, 1);
    expect(hex(mapColor24(w.store, 0, 0))).toBe('#91B9FF');
    w.store.setLiquid(0, 0, 33, 1);
    expect(hex(mapColor24(w.store, 0, 0))).toBe('#093DBF');
  });
});

function grid2x2(colors: Array<[number, number, number, number]>): ArtGrid {
  // 2×2 RGBA 手工构造(绕开 DOM canvas):colors 顺序 [tl, tr, bl, br]
  const rgba = new Uint8ClampedArray(16);

  return { w: 2, h: 2, rgba };
}

      if (r.errors.length) console.warn(`[atlas-lint] ${r.errors.length} error —— 构建将阻断`);

// 玩家储物（bank×4：存钱罐/保险箱/守护者熔炉/虚空保险库）持久化测试
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { saveGame, loadSave } from '../src/save/SaveFile';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

describe('玩家储物 bank×4', () => {
  it('默认 4 组 × 40 槽全空', () => {
    const p = new Player(0, 0, new Inventory());
    expect(p.banks.length).toBe(4);
    for (const b of p.banks) {
      expect(b.length).toBe(40);
      expect(b.every((s) => s === null)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('saveGame → loadSave 往返：bank 内容完整保留（旧档缺省兼容全空）', () => {
    const w = new World(60, 40, 42, 'bank-test');
    const p = new Player(100, 100, new Inventory());
    p.banks[0][0] = { id: 1, stack: 99 };   // 存钱罐
    p.banks[1][39] = { id: 2, stack: 50 };  // 保险箱末槽
    p.banks[2][20] = { id: 3, stack: 7 };   // 守护者熔炉
    p.banks[3][5] = { id: 4, stack: 1 };    // 虚空保险库
    const json = saveGame(w, p, 0);

    const { player } = loadSave(json);
    expect(player.banks?.[0][0]).toEqual({ id: 1, stack: 99 });
    expect(player.banks?.[1][39]).toEqual({ id: 2, stack: 50 });
    expect(player.banks?.[2][20]).toEqual({ id: 3, stack: 7 });
    expect(player.banks?.[3][5]).toEqual({ id: 4, stack: 1 });
    expect(player.banks?.[3][6]).toBeNull();
    // 旧档兼容：手工剥掉 banks 字段
    const raw = JSON.parse(json) as { player: Record<string, unknown> };

```
