// 同值不重复触发（幂等）\n    expect(hits.length).toBe(2);\n  });\n\n  it('KillTile 清漆（WorldGen.cs:63933-63939）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(4, 4);\n    st.setTileSilent(1, 1, DIRT);\n    st.setPaintSilent(1, 1, 5);\n    st.setTile(1, 1, 0);\n    expect(st.paint[st.idx(1, 1)]).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('PlaceTile 在非活性格上清漆（:59499-59501）；active→active 替换保留漆', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(4, 4);\n    // 空格遗留漆（模拟导入脏数据）→ 放块即清\n    st.setPaintSilent(1, 1, 5);\n    st.setTileSilent(1, 1, DIRT);\n    expect(st.paint[st.idx(1, 1)]).toBe(0);\n    // 已活性格带漆 → 换型保留（草化/转化路径）\n    st.setPaintSilent(1, 1, 7);\n    st.setTileSilent(1, 1, STONE);\n    expect(st.paint[st.idx(1, 1)]).toBe(7);\n  });\n\n  it('KillWall 清墙漆（WorldGen.cs:60425-60426）；铺新墙从零开始', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(4, 4);\n    st.setWallSilent(1, 1, 4);\n    st.setPaintWallSilent(1, 1, 9);\n    st.setWallSilent(1, 1, 0);\n    expect(st.paintWall[st.idx(1, 1)]).toBe(0);\n    st.setWallSilent(1, 1, 15);\n    expect(st.paintWall[st.idx(1, 1)]).toBe(0);\n    st.setPaintWallSilent(1, 1, 3);\n    st.setWallSilent(1, 1, 16);   // wall→wall 替换保留\n    expect(st.paintWall[st.idx(1, 1)]).toBe(3);\n  });\n\n  it('setPaint 钳 5bit 域（0-31，越界值 &31）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(2, 2);\n    st.setPaint(0, 0, 33);\n    expect(st.paint[st.idx(0, 0)]).toBe(1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('存档往返（SaveData.paint/paintWall 独立 RLE 通道）', () => {\n  it('saveGame → loadSave：方块漆/墙漆完整保留', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42, 'paint-test');\n    const st = w.store;\n    st.setTileSilent(10, 10, DIRT);\n    st.setPaintSilent(10, 10, 5);\n    st.setWallSilent(11, 11, 4);\n    st.setPaintWallSilent(11, 11, 9);\n    const p = new Player(100, 100, new Inventory());\n    const back = loadSave(saveGame(w, p, 0));\n    const st2 = back.world.store;\n    expect(st2.paint[st2.idx(10, 10)]).toBe(5);\n    expect(st2.paintWall[st2.idx(11, 11)]).toBe(9);\n    // 未涂格保持 0\n    expect(st2.paint[st2.idx(20, 20)]).toBe(0);\n    expect(st2.paintWall[st2.idx(20, 20)]).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('旧档缺 paint/paintWall 字段 → 全 0 无漆（同 wire/blocks 缺省容忍）', () => {\n    const w = new World(60, 40, 42, 'paint-old');\n    w.store.setTileSilent(5, 5, DIRT);\n    const json = saveGame(w, new Player(0, 0, new Inventory()), 0);\n    const raw = JSON.parse(json) as Record<string, unknown>;\n    delete raw.paint;\n    delete raw.paintWall;\n    const back = loadSave(JSON.stringify(raw));\n    const st = back.world.store;\n    expect(st.paint.every((v) => v === 0)).toBe(true);\n    expect(st.paintWall.every((v) => v === 0)).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('Item.paint / paintTool 表（Item.cs:13374-13535 / :21168-21185）', () => {\n  it('油漆 1073-1099 → PaintID 1-27（含深层 13-24）', () => {\n    expect(itemOf(1073)?.paint).toBe(1);   // RedPaint\n    expect(itemOf(1084)?.paint).toBe(12);  // PinkPaint\n    expect(itemOf(1085)?.paint).toBe(13);  // DeepRedPaint\n    expect(itemOf(1096)?.paint).toBe(24);  // DeepPinkPaint\n    expect(itemOf(1097)?.paint).toBe(25);  // BlackPaint\n    expect(itemOf(1098)?.paint).toBe(26);  // WhitePaint\n    expect(itemOf(1099)?.paint).toBe(27);  // GrayPaint\n  });\n\n  it('棕/暗影/负相 1966-1968 → 28/29/30', () => {\n    expect(itemOf(1966)?.paint).toBe(28);\n    expect(itemOf(1967)?.paint).toBe(29);\n    expect(itemOf(1968)?.paint).toBe(30);\n  });\n\n  it('工具族 paintTool（Player.cs PlaceThing 分派）', () => {\n    expect(itemOf(1071)?.paintTool).toBe('brush');\n    expect(itemOf(1543)?.paintTool).toBe('brush');    // SpectrePaintbrush\n    expect(itemOf(1072)?.paintTool).toBe('roller');\n    expect(itemOf(1544)?.paintTool).toBe('roller');\n    expect(itemOf(1100)?.paintTool).toBe('scraper');\n    expect(itemOf(1545)?.paintTool).toBe('scraper');\n    expect(itemOf(1071)?.paint).toBeUndefined();      // 工具无 paint\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('FindPaintOrCoating（Player.cs:41272-41289）', () => {\n  it('弹药栏 54-57 优先于背包（:41274-41280 先扫弹药栏）', () => {\n    const inv = new Inventory();\n    const red = ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[1073]];\n    const blue = ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[1081]];\n    inv.add(blue, 10);        // 进 0-49 背包\n    inv.slots[54] = { id: red, stack: 5 };\n    expect(inv.findPaintSlot()).toBe(54);\n    expect(inv.heldPaintLookup()).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  it('无弹药栏油漆时回落全包首槽（:41281-41287）', () => {\n    const inv = new Inventory();\n    const blue = ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[1081]];\n    inv.add(blue, 3);\n    const slot = inv.findPaintSlot();\n    expect(slot).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n    expect(slot).toBeLessThan(50);\n    expect(inv.heldPaintLookup()).toBe(9);            // BluePaint → PaintID 9\n  });\n\n  it('非油漆物品不被命中（Item.PaintOrCoating :334-344）', () => {\n    const inv = new Inventory();\n    inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[2]], 99); // 泥土\n    expect(inv.findPaintSlot()).toBe(-1);\n    expect(inv.heldPaintLookup()).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('SmartCursor 油漆三策略（SmartCursorHelper.cs:668-807）', () => {\n  const brush = () => ({ def: itemOf(1071) ?? null, tool: null });\n  const roller = () => ({ def: itemOf(1072) ?? null, tool: null });\n  const scraper = () => ({ def: itemOf(1100) ?? null, tool: null });\n\n  function paintField(st: TileStore, w: number, h: number, y: number): void {\n    for (let x = 60; x < 60 + w; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, y, DIRT);\n    for (let y2 = y + 1; y2 < y + 1 + h; y2++) {\n      for (let x = 60; x < 60 + w; x++) st.setWallSilent(x, y2, 4);\n    }\n  }\n\n  it('Step_PaintBrush：无油漆（paintLookup=0）→ 目标集恒空（:724 外门）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(220, 160);\n    paintField(st, 20, 4, 90);\n    expect(scRun(st, mouseAt(70, 90), brush())).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it('Step_PaintBrush：选离鼠标最近且颜色 != 目标漆的活性格（:733-743）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(220, 160);\n    paintField(st, 20, 4, 90);\n    st.setPaintSilent(70, 90, 1);   // 已是目标色 → 排除\n    const hit = scRun(st, mouseAt(70, 90), brush(), { paintLookup: 1 });\n    expect(hit).not.toBeNull();\n    expect(hit!.x).not.toBe(70);    // 同色格不可选\n    // 鼠标正好在 (71,90) → 最近候选是它\n    const hit2 = scRun(st, mouseAt(71, 90), brush(), { paintLookup: 1 });\n    expect(hit2).toEqual({ x: 71, y: 90 });\n  });\n\n  it('Step_PaintRoller：墙色 != 目标漆且墙前无实心块（:781）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(220, 160);\n    paintField(st, 20, 4, 90);\n    st.setPaintWallSilent(68, 91, 9); // 已同色 → 排除\n    const hit = scRun(st, mouseAt(68, 91), roller(), { paintLookup: 9 });\n    expect(hit).not.toBeNull();\n    expect(hit).toEqual({ x: 67, y: 91 }); // (67,91) 比 (69,91) 近（68 被排除）\n  });\n\n  it('Step_PaintRoller：实心块挡住的墙格不可选（:781 !active || !tileSolid || tileSolidTop）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(220, 160);\n    for (let x = 60; x < 80; x++) st.setWallSilent(x, 92, 4);\n    st.setTileSilent(70, 92, DIRT);   // 实心块正面 → 排除\n    const hit = scRun(st, mouseAt(70, 92), roller(), { paintLookup: 9 });\n    expect(hit).not.toBeNull();\n    expect(hit!.x).not.toBe(70);\n  });\n\n  it('Step_PaintScrapper：有漆格/墙漆格为目标（:681-693）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(220, 160);\n    paintField(st, 20, 4, 90);\n    st.setPaintSilent(66, 90, 5);\n    st.setPaintWallSilent(74, 92, 3);\n    const a = scRun(st, mouseAt(66, 90), scraper());\n    expect(a).toEqual({ x: 66, y: 90 });\n    const b = scRun(st, mouseAt(74, 92), scraper());\n    expect(b).toEqual({ x: 74, y: 92 });\n    // 无漆区 → 无目标\n    const c = scRun(st, mouseAt(100, 95), scraper());\n    expect(c).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it('Step_PaintScrapper：长苔藓 sheet 184 也是目标（:684 tile.type == 184）', () => {\n    const st = new TileStore(220, 160);\n    paintField(st, 20, 4, 90);\n    const moss = TILE_BY_KEY['v_184_moss_plants'];\n    if (moss === undefined) return; // tile 未注册时跳过（等价于原版不可达态）\n    st.setTileSilent(62, 89, moss);\n    const hit = scRun(st, mouseAt(62, 89), scraper());\n    expect(hit).toEqual({ x: 62, y: 89 });\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('史莱姆踩漆 lerp（NPC.cs:60971-61087）', () => {\n  it('站在红漆上：颜色向 (255,0,0) 收敛、alpha 向 100 收敛（:60977-60981）', () => {\n    const w = new World(64, 48, 7, 'slime-paint');\n    for (let x = 28; x < 36; x++) w.store.setTileSilent(x, 30, DIRT);\n    w.store.setPaintSilent(30, 30, 1);   // 红漆\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(1, 30 * TILE, 28 * TILE); // 绿史莱姆（type 1）\n    expect(e).not.toBeNull();\n    expect(e!.colorRGBA).not.toBeNull();\n    const before = [...e!.colorRGBA!];\n    const p = new Player(4 * TILE, 20 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const h = hooks(w, p);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 240 && !e!.dead; i++) e!.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h);\n    const after = e!.colorRGBA!;\n    // 至少发生过一次 (c*19+target)/20 迈向 (255,0,0)\n    expect(after[0]).toBeGreaterThan(before[0]);\n    expect(after[1]).toBeLessThan(before[1]);\n    expect(after[3]).toBeLessThan(before[3]);\n  });\n\n  it('负相漆 30 不触发 lerp（:60977 color() != 30 门）', () => {\n    const w = new World(64, 48, 7, 'slime-neg');\n    for (let x = 28; x < 36; x++) w.store.setTileSilent(x, 30, DIRT);\n    w.store.setPaintSilent(30, 30, 30);\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(1, 30 * TILE, 28 * TILE)!;\n    const before = [...e.colorRGBA!];\n    const h = hooks(w, new Player(4 * TILE, 20 * TILE, new Inventory()));\n    for (let i = 0; i < 120 && !e.dead; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h);\n    // 负相漆不吃：R 不向 200 收敛（无 ai[1] 物品色，颜色应基本不变）\n    expect(Math.abs(e.colorRGBA![0] - before[0])).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);\n  });\n\n  it('无漆立足点 → 不变色（无 ai[1] 体内物品时全静默）', () => {\n    const w = new World(64, 48, 7, 'slime-plain');\n    for (let x = 28; x < 36; x++) w.store.setTileSilent(x, 30, DIRT);\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(1, 30 * TILE, 28 * TILE)!;\n    const before = [...e.colorRGBA!];\n    const h = hooks(w, new Player(4 * TILE, 20 * TILE, new Inventory()));\n    for (let i = 0; i < 120 && !e.dead; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h);\n    expect(e.colorRGBA![0]).toBeCloseTo(before[0], 5);\n    expect(e.colorRGBA![1]).toBeCloseTo(before[1], 5);\n  });\n\n  it('油漆格不实心（空气/平台）不算立足点（SolidTile3 :70470-70489）', () => {\n    const w = new World(64, 48, 7, 'slime-nonsolid');\n    // 立足行全空、只给一格平台刷漆（tileSolidTop 不算）\n    const st = w.store;\n    const plat = TILE_BY_KEY['platform'];\n    if (plat === undefined) return;\n    for (let x = 28; x < 36; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, 31, DIRT); // 底下一行实心\n    st.setTileSilent(30, 30, plat);\n    st.setPaintSilent(30, 30, 1);\n    st.setPaintSilent(31, 30, 1); // 空气格遗留漆\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(1, 30 * TILE, 28 * TILE)!;\n    const before = [...e.colorRGBA!];\n    const h = hooks(w, new Player(4 * TILE, 20 * TILE, new Inventory()));\n    for (let i = 0; i < 120 && !e.dead; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h);\n    expect(Math.abs(e.colorRGBA![0] - before[0])).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('油漆师商店链（Chest.SetupShop case 15 :2416-2455 → 我方 vanilla-shopstock）', () => {\n  it('227 主店进货：三件套 + 常驻 15 漆 + hardMode 门 1967/1968 + 墓地门 4668/5344', async () => {\n    const j = (await import('../src/data/vanilla-shopstock.json')).default as {\n      shops: Record<string, Array<{ id: number; cond: string | null }>>;\n    };\n    const ids = j.shops['227'].map((e) => e.id);\n    expect(ids).toContain(1071);   // Paintbrush :2418\n    expect(ids).toContain(1072);   // PaintRoller :2420\n    expect(ids).toContain(1100);   // PaintScraper :2422\n    expect(ids).toContain(1073);   // RedPaint（1073-1084 循环 :2424-2428）\n    expect(ids).toContain(1084);\n    expect(ids).toContain(1097);   // Black :2429\n    expect(ids).toContain(1099);   // Gray :2431\n    expect(ids).toContain(1098);   // White :2433\n    expect(ids).toContain(1966);   // Brown :2435\n    expect(ids).toContain(1967);   // Shadow（hardMode 门 :2437-2441）\n    expect(ids).toContain(1968);   // Negative（同上）\n    expect(ids).toContain(4668);   // 荧光涂层（墓地门 :2444-2446）\n    expect(ids).toContain(5344);   // 回声涂层（墓地+世花门 :2448-2450）\n    // 全部进货物品都在注册表里（npcShopBuy 的 ITEM_BY_KEY 门可过）\n    for (const id of ids) {\n      expect(VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id]).toBeDefined();\n      expect(ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id]]).toBeDefined();\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('油漆物品可入弹药格（Item.FitsAmmoSlot :1351-1367 PaintOrCoating 豁免）', async () => {\n    const { ammoSlotAccepts } = await import('../src/items/Inventory');\n    expect(ammoSlotAccepts(ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[1073]])).toBe(true);\n    expect(ammoSlotAccepts(ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[1968]])).toBe(true);\n    expect(ammoSlotAccepts(ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[2]])).toBe(false); // 泥土不收\n  });\n\n  it('tile 定义消费就绪：DIRT/STONE 有 mapColor 供 Minimap LUT（vanillaMapColors 链）', () => {\n    expect(TILE_DEFS[DIRT]?.vanilla?.sheet).toBeDefined();\n    expect(TILE_DEFS[STONE]?.vanilla?.sheet).toBeDefined();\n  });\n});\n"
```json
}
```

```
3. SmartCursor strategies return null. Why? The scan region (reach) is around the actor at (100,95); my paint field is at x=60-80, y=90-94. Reach = tileRegion(actor, 0, st) → x range roughly 100±5, y 95±3. So x=60..80 is OUT of reach! I need to place the field near the actor: x 95-105, y 90-94. Let me recompute: actor x=100*16 → tile 100; y=95*16 → tile 95. Reach x0 = floor(1600/16) - 5 = 95, x1 = ceil((1600+20)/16)-1+5 = 106; y0 = floor(1520/16)-3 = 92, y1 = ceil((1520+42)/16)-1+3 = 99. So reach x: 95..106, y: 92..99.

Let me restructure: tiles row at y=95 (actor row), walls below at y=96+. And mouse at those coords. Let me redo helper:
```

```json
 "new_string": "describe('油漆师商店链（Chest.SetupShop case 15 :2416-2455 → 我方 vanilla-shopstock）', () => {\n  it('227 主店进货：三件套 + 常驻黑灰白棕 + hardMode 门 1967/1968 + 墓地门 4668/5344', async () => {\n    const j = (await import('../src/data/vanilla-shopstock.json')).default as {\n      shops: Record<string, Array<{ id: number; cond: string | null }>>;\n    };\n    const stock = j.shops['227'];\n    const ids = stock.map((e) => e.id);\n    expect(ids).toContain(1071);   // Paintbrush :2418\n    expect(ids).toContain(1072);   // PaintRoller :2420\n    expect(ids).toContain(1100);   // PaintScraper :2422\n    expect(ids).toContain(1097);   // Black :2429\n    expect(ids).toContain(1099);   // Gray :2431\n    expect(ids).toContain(1098);   // White :2433\n    expect(ids).toContain(1966);   // Brown :2435\n    expect(ids).toContain(1967);   // Shadow（hardMode 门 :2437-2441）\n    expect(ids).toContain(1968);   // Negative（同上）\n    expect(ids).toContain(4668);   // 荧光涂层（墓地门 :2444-2446）\n    expect(ids).toContain(5344);   // 回声涂层（墓地+世花门 :2448-2450）\n    // 1073-1084 基础 12 色是 for 循环上架（:2424-2428），extract-shops 不展开 →\n    // 由 Game.shopStockFor 直补（见 shopStockFor 内 227 分支）；此处锁全部进货可购买\n    for (const id of [...ids, ...Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => 1073 + i)]) {\n      expect(VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id]).toBeDefined();\n      expect(ITEM_BY_KEY[VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[id]]).toBeDefined();\n    }\n    // hardMode 门挂对条目（Shadow/Negative 仅困难模式上架）\n    expect(stock.find((e) => e.id === 1967)?.cond).toBe('hardMode');\n    expect(stock.find((e) => e.id === 1968)?.cond).toBe('hardMode');\n  });"
}
```

````
The implementation looks intact. Two fidelity concerns to reconsider:

1. **Brush/roller consume useTime even when no paint is found** — vanilla: PlaceThing_Paintbrush requires active tile, then `if (ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem) TryPainting(...)`. The itemTime/itemAnimation are set by the general item-use path regardless of paint presence. So yes, using the brush with no paint still "uses" (swings + cooldown). My implementation matches.

2. **The brush requires `p.useTime === 0`** — in our code, useTime is the item cooldown decremented elsewhere. Vanilla: the use triggers at itemTime==0 && autoReuse → sets itemTime=useTime. Then PlaceThing requires ItemTimeIsZero... wait, if the use sets itemTime=10 immediately, then ItemTimeIsZero is false in the same frame, and the paint would never happen?!

Let me think again about vanilla sequencing within one frame (Player.ItemCheck):
- itemTime-- each frame (in ItemCheck via `itemTime--`? Actually itemTime is decremented in Player.Update).
- When controlUseItem && itemTime == 0: ItemCheck's use logic runs: sets itemAnimation = useAnimation (15) and itemTime = useTime? Let me check: in vanilla, `ItemCheck` has:
```
if (itemTime == 0 && controlUseItem ...) { ... itemAnimation = sItem.useAnimation; ... itemTime = sItem.useTime ... }
```
Hmm, actually vanilla sets `itemTime = sItem.useTime` only via ApplyItemTime in specific paths. The general use: `ItemCheck_ApplyUseStyle` sets itemAnimation; `itemTime` is set by `ApplyItemTime` called from various use handlers... 

For paint tools specifically: who sets itemTime? PlaceThing_Paintbrush → TryPainting → ApplyPaint → ApplyItemTime on success. So the FIRST use of a paint brush: itemAnimation set by the swing (useStyle 1), itemTime stays 0 → PlaceThing's `ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 && controlUseItem` is TRUE → TryPainting runs → ApplyPaint succeeds → ApplyItemTime(useTime=10) → cooldown. Then for 10 ticks itemTime > 0 → no paint. At itemTime==0 again (and itemAnimation still > 0? itemAnimation=15 > itemTime=10, so after 10 ticks itemAnimation has 5 left) → ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation > 0 → paint again!

That's the vanilla loop: paint every 10 ticks while held, as long as the swing animation is still running. If the player releases and re-presses, itemAnimation restarts.

So my implementation (useTime===0 → swing + useTime=10 + paint) reproduces exactly this cadence: paint on the frame useTime hits 0. ✓. And with autoReuse, holding keeps the swing going. One difference: vanilla requires itemAnimation > 0; if the player holds the brush without a target... the swing would have ended (itemAnimation reaches 0 after 15 ticks) and then no paint until re-click. In my version, useTime===0 && mouseDown → always re-triggers swing + attempts paint. This means holding on an invalid target keeps swinging — minor divergence, same class as vanilla autoReuse behavior. Acceptable; documented cadence is exact.

3. `p.useTime = 0` in the scraper's unreachable branch — harmless.

Now, one more important check: does the use-dispatch code path actually reach my paint branch when the item is a paint tool? The earlier branches: melee (cwMelee && !tool) — paint tools have no combat data? `combatWeapon(heldDef)` — do paint tools appear in vanilla-itemcombat.json as melee? Item.cs for 1071: useStyle 1, no melee=true → not a weapon. So cwMelee null → skip. tool = heldDef?.tool ?? itemFuncTool — paint tools have no tool field → null. Then extractinator (needs target 219/642) → skip. Then MY paint branch. ✓ Then wireTool/wallId/tile — paint items have no wallId/tile. ✓

Also `itemFuncTool(held.id)` might classify... it returns pick/axe/hammer for tools; paint tools aren't. OK.

4. One concern: my dispatch `if (heldDef?.paintTool && inp.mouseDown && !inp.rightDown)` is placed AFTER the extractinator check but BEFORE wireTool — matches PlaceThing order (paint before tiles/walls). ✓

Now let me also double check the SmartCursor brush test passed with correct reasoning, and think about whether `sem.paintLookup` being 0 for scraper is right — yes (vanilla TryFindingPaintInplayerInventory only for 1071/1543/1072/1544).
````

```
310	/** tiles 段解码：列优先 + header 链 + RLE */
311	function decodeTiles(
312	  r: BinaryReaderLE, w: number, h: number,
313	  version: number, frameImportant: boolean[],
314	): WldTileData {
315	  const n = w * h;
316	  const active = new Uint8Array(n);
317	  const type = new Uint16Array(n);
318	  const frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
319	  const frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
320	  const wall = new Uint16Array(n);
321	  const liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
322	  const liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
323	  const wire = new Uint8Array(n);
324	  const half = new Uint8Array(n);
325	  const slope = new Uint8Array(n);
326	  const halfBits = half, slopeBits = slope; // 半砖/坡面解码目标
327	
328	  let idx = 0; // y * w + x，列优先推进
329	  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
330	    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
331	      const header1 = r.u8();
332	      let header2 = 0, header3 = 0, header4 = 0;
333	      if (header1 & 0x01) header2 = r.u8();
334	      if (header2 & 0x01) header3 = r.u8();
335	      if (version >= 269 && header3 & 0x01) header4 = r.u8();
336	      void header4;
337	
338	      let tileType = 0;
339	      let fx = 0, fy = 0;
340	      if (header1 & 0x02) {
341	        // active
342	        if (header1 & 0x20) {
343	          tileType = r.u16();
344	        } else {
345	          tileType = r.u8();
346	        }
347	        const isFramed = tileType < frameImportant.length ? frameImportant[tileType] : true;
348	        if (isFramed) {
349	          fx = r.u16();
350	          fy = r.u16();
351	        }
352	        if (header3 & 0x08) r.u8(); // tile color
353	      }
354	      let wl = 0;
355	      if (header1 & 0x04) {
356	        wl = r.u8();
357	        if (header3 & 0x10) r.u8(); // wall color
358	        if (version >= 222 && header3 & 0x40) {
359	          wl |= r.u8() << 8;
360	        }
361	      }
362	      // 电路位（1456/1405 同布局,WorldFile.cs:2610-2656）：header2 0x02红/0x04蓝/0x08绿,
363	      // header3 0x02致动器/0x04已致动/0x20黄线（1.4 起致动器与黄线在 header3;
364	      // 旧写法 0x3E>>1 是 1.3 布局——0x10/0x20 已是 half/slope 位,会漏进黄线/致动器）
365	      let wireBits = (header2 & 0x0E) >> 1;
366	      if (header3 & 0x02) wireBits |= 0x10; // 致动器
367	      if (header3 & 0x04) wireBits |= 0x20; // 已致动(inActive)
368	      if (header3 & 0x20) wireBits |= 0x08; // 黄线
369	      // half/slope(WorldFile.cs:1519):(header2 >> 4) & 7 → 1=半砖,2-5=slope1-4
370	      const blockBits = (header2 >> 4) & 7;
371	      const blockHalf = blockBits === 1 ? 1 : 0;
372	      const blockSlope = blockBits >= 2 && blockBits <= 5 ? blockBits - 1 : 0;
373	      const lqType = (header1 & 0x18) >> 3;
374	      let lqAmount = 0, lqKind = 0;
375	      if (lqType !== 0) {
376	        lqAmount = r.u8();
377	        lqKind = (version >= 269 && header3 & 0x80) ? 1 : lqType;
378	      }
379	      // RLE 计数
380	      const rleStorage = (header1 & 0xC0) >> 6;
381	      let rle = 0;
382	      if (rleStorage === 1) rle = r.u8();
383	      else if (rleStorage >= 2) rle = r.u16();
384	
385	      // 写入本格 + RLE 重复格（列优先向下）
386	      for (let k = 0; k <= rle && y + k < h; k++) {
387	        const i = (y + k) * w + x;
388	        active[i] = (header1 & 0x02) ? 1 : 0;
389	        type[i] = tileType;
390	        frameX[i] = fx;
391	        frameY[i] = fy;
392	        wall[i] = wl;
393	        liquid[i] = lqAmount;
394	        liquidType[i] = lqKind;

```

```
油漆（Paint）系统全量移植完成。以下是完整报告。

## 一、原版调研结论（行号 = ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/）

| 链路 | 原版位置 | 关键语义 |
|---|---|---|
| 存储位 | Tile.cs:609-617（方块漆 = `sTileHeader & 0x1F`）、:365-373（墙漆 = `bTileHeader & 0x1F`） | 双通道各 5bit（0-31） |
| PaintID 全表 | Terraria.ID/PaintID.cs:5-69 | 1-12 浅层 / 13-24 深层 / 25 黑 26 白 27 灰 28 棕 29 暗影 30 负相 / **31 废弃**（1.4.4 起改涂层体系，WorldFile.cs:2806-2825 迁移） |
| paintColor | WorldGen.cs:43948-44061 | 深层 13-24 与浅层**同 RGB**；负相 30=(200,200,200,**150**)；0/31 → White |
| 涂刷原语 | WorldGen.cs:44137-44159 paintTile / 44257-44279 paintWall | 守卫：非活性/无墙/同色 → false；尘效 tile 先于写入、wall 后于写入 |
| 交互三件套 | Player.cs:38923-38925 分派序、:41254-41270 刷、:41236-41252 滚、:41152-41234 刮（含 LongMoss :41166-41213）、:41291-41369 TryPainting/ApplyPaint | **通道由工具决定**（刷=tile/滚=wall），Item 无 paintWall 字段；每次涂 1 格扣 1 个，同色不扣；useTime 10 × tileSpeed/wallSpeed（:4221-4230）；Spectre 1543-1545 tileBoost+3（Item.cs:18571-18583） |
| 找漆 | Player.cs:41272-41289 FindPaintOrCoating | 弹药栏 54-57 优先，再全包 0-57 |
| 智能光标三策略 | SmartCursorHelper.cs:668-720 刮 / :722-765 刷 / :767-807 滚，油漆 lookup 装配 :167-181 | 全部“扫描可达区 → 离鼠标最近”；滚刷要求墙前无实心块（:781） |
| 小地图换色 | MapHelper.cs:1812-1863 MapColor、:1865-1882 GetMapTileXnaColor、:1993-2005 墙豁免、:1965-1968 tile 160 豁免 | 默认分支 = paintColor × **最大通道**；暗影 29 用中位通道×0.3；负相 30 墙半幅反转 |
| 史莱姆踩漆 | NPC.cs:60971-61087 | 底边下一格 SolidTile3（:70470-70489）&& 漆>0 && !=30 → (c*19+p)/20、alpha 目标 100；踩漆时体内物品色不生效 |
| 商店 | Chest.cs:2416-2455 case 15（Painter=NPC 227） | 1073-1084 为 **for 循环上架**（:2424-2428），extract-shops.mjs 未展开 |

## 二、实现清单（我方落点，全部行号注释）

- **`src/world/Paint.ts`（新建）**：PAINT_ID 枚举（:77 paintColor 逐行 1:1、:105 coatingColor、:131 applyPaintTint、:147 mapPaintColor 1:1、:62 PAINT_RGB 预展开、:206/:220 paintTile/paintWall 原语 + PaintDustHook）。
- **TileStore.ts**：`paint`/`paintWall` Uint8Array + buffer 注入（旧包缺省补零）+ setPaint/setPaintWall（发 listeners 驱动 chunk/小地图重建）；setTile/setTileSilent 按 KillTile(:63933-63939)/PlaceTile(:59499-59501) 清漆；setWall 按 KillWall(:60425-60426) 清墙漆。
- **存档往返**：serialize.ts 独立 RLE 通道 `paint`/`paintWall`（同 wire/blocks 缺省容忍模式）+ SaveFile.ts 解码（&31 钳域）+ save.worker/SaveClient/worldPacket/protocol TileBuffers 全链，server persist.ts 走 saveWorldOnly 自动携带。
- **.wld 导入**：WldParser.ts 此前读到油漆字节即丢弃（:352/:357），现落盘到 WldTileData；WldImport.ts 直拷进 SaveData。
- **交互**：Game.ts:6470 tryUsePaintTool（三件套 + LongMoss 1/9 苔藓掉落 + Spectre 射程）、:6566 tryPainting（消耗语义）、:6589/:6594 tileSpeed/wallSpeed；分派置于电线工具前（PlaceThing 序）。Inventory.ts findPaintSlot/heldPaintLookup（弹药栏优先）。items.ts ItemDef.paint/paintTool + 1073-1099→1-27、1966-1968→28-30、工具族表。
- **智能光标**：SmartCursor.ts:1374/:1406/:1433 三策略从 no-op 激活，env.paintLookup 装配（:167-181 对应）。
- **渲染**：ChunkCache.ts:38 tintRegion（multiply/difference + destination-in 三段合成）、:392 油漆 pass（墙/tile 双层）；Renderer.ts 小地图 colorFor + redrawAll 热循环 ABGR 直算（:637）+ 豁免表（:501）。
- **Enemy.ts:1316**：slimeColorTick 踩漆扫描激活——**并修正原误挂**（此前挂在 zombieAI，守卫 vanillaId!==1 使其全程空转，移回 slimeAI）。
- **商店**：shopStockFor 补 1073-1084 循环段（extract-shops 同类缺口，与 4702/moonPhase 既有补丁同模式）。

## 三、等价边界（登记，非遗漏）

1. **tile 渲染乘色系数**：原版在编译 effect（游戏资源 TileShader.cso，DXBC）内，反编译 C# 不可见（仅可见 pass 选择 Main.cs:58064-58083）。Canvas 2D 按通行乘色模型实现；**深层漆 13-24 渲染等价于浅层**（数据 1:1，pass 强度差异未移植）。曾尝试取 tModLoader shader 源失败（其仓库亦只含编译产物）。
2. **涂层（paintCoating 4668 荧光/5344 回声）系统未建**：无 fullbright/invisible 存储/渲染位，涂层物品惰性（商店可买、无效果）；刮刀/三策略中涂层条件项恒 false。
3. **ChunkCache 按 16×16 格区域着色**，原版按绘制调用（多格家具整张贴图随锚格上色）——涂多格物件只有被涂格区域显色。
4. **油漆不上 TileOp 多人同步**（协议枚举固定，改则破坏客户端兼容）。
5. 存档采用独立 RLE 通道而非扩展 rleTiles 条目格式——与 wire/blocks 同模式，保旧档兼容（原版语义对应 WF:1474-1491 独立字节位）。

## 四、验证结果
```
